## VOLUME 08 BATCH 0002 IS REPAIRED A SECOND TIME — Chapters 361–370, *The Doors*. **THE BAND'S RECEIPT AFTER A SECOND REVIEW AND A SECOND REPAIR. This block supersedes the `## VOLUME 08 BATCH 0002 IS REPAIRED` block below it and nothing below it has been edited. THE BAND STANDS: same ten chapters, same titles, same plot, same findings, same refusals, same ending, and not one character's decision changed. What changed is one division, one mislabelled subtraction, one duplicated clause, one list that was summarising the chapter before it, one name that had gone missing, one quotation mark, and the record's own instrument figures. The receipt is `reviews/volume-08/batch-0002.md` and the findings are `logs/batch-0002.review.log`.**

### WHAT THE REVIEW FOUND, IN ONE PARAGRAPH

**FIVE CRITICAL. `0363:21` divided nine hundred by two and printed four hundred and twenty, and that is the spine figure of the entire volume and was being handed to the next band as canon with the bad working attached. `0365:29` called this ward's two hundred names twelve shillings, which is the three-hundred figure, and put a hundred and thirty-eight pence beside it, which is the subtraction that belongs to the three hundred. `0370` said ten days in one breath and eleven in another twelve lines down. `0364:69` put a clerk of forty in a room the clerk of thirty describes being in at `0361:49`. And `0370:21–33` restated `0369:73–79` line for line while `0370:29` repeated a clause from `0369:61` near-verbatim. FOUR MAJOR. Two more of the day-count class the first repair had claimed to close, plus one it had not found, which is `362`'s *nine days ago* for a scene that happened seven days ago; and Fenna Rusk named once at `360` and then never named in the ten chapters where she is the subject; and the next band's prompt contradicting itself on its own span figures, its word count, and its numbering; and two figures in the previous repair's own record that the chapter files disprove. FIVE MINOR, four of them real and one of them the ledger, which is controller-owned and was left alone. That is the sixth time this project has reviewed a band and found that the prose is sound and the record of the prose is not.**

### WHAT THE REPAIR DID TO THE ARITHMETIC, AND IT IS TWO THINGS

**THE ROUND IS THIRTY LOTS OF A FORTNIGHT. It is now said that way in the sentence that made the number, with the fraction in it — two doors a day and a seventh — and the woman says she has never once rounded it, and the two other places in that chapter that flattened the rate to *two doors a day* were changed to match. 420, 364, 56, a year and fifty-six days, 234, and 12⅖ days of a round are all untouched, because none of them was wrong. `355` already had the multiplication and is now cited as the authority in the next band's prompt, which carried the bad working forward as canon.**

**A DIFFERENCE MUST NAME ITS TWO TERMS. 138 is 282 − 144, the three hundred names at twelve shillings. 186 is 282 − 96, this ward's own two hundred at eight shillings. 234 is 282 − 48, a woman's year, in her own mouth. Three subtractions off one base of 282 is not a contradiction; a subtraction whose terms the sentence does not identify is. All three are now named in the same breath, and all three are spent.**

### WHAT THE REPAIR DID TO THE PROSE, AND IT IS FIVE THINGS AND NONE OF THEM IS A PLOT

**A LIST THAT WAS SUMMARISING THE CHAPTER BEFORE IT NOW COUNTS THE FOUR THINGS THAT CAME OUT OF THE FOURTEENTH, in half the length, with two facts the old list did not have, and the word *anchor* held at two. THE NAME IS BACK, TWICE, AND BOTH TIMES IT IS WHERE THE COST OF PUTTING IT THERE IS FELT AND NEITHER TIME IS A REVELATION. AN UNCLOSED QUOTATION IN THE CLERK'S ONE-WORD REFUSAL IS CLOSED, A STRAIGHT QUOTE INSIDE A QUOTATION IS GONE, AND EVERY LINE IN ALL TEN FILES NOW BALANCES. THE WORD *CHAPTER* AND THE PHRASE *FOR THE REST OF THE VOLUME* ARE BOTH OUT, AND THEY WERE THE LAST TWO PLACES THE NARRATOR WAS STANDING OUTSIDE HIS OWN BOOK. AND FOUR OF THE SIX CLOSINGS THAT RAN THE SAME BELLS-DID-NOT-STOP LITANY ARE NOW FOUR, WITH THE MOTIF OPENING THE BAND AT `361` AND CLOSING IT AT `370`.**

### WHAT THE REPAIR DID TO THE RECORD, AND IT IS FOUR THINGS

**The next band's prompt had Batch 0001's span figures sitting in the same sentence as the right ones, a word count one high, *fifteen* where the figure is *sixteen*, and a list numbered 1–6, 7, 7, 9. All four are corrected against the chapter files, and the money table now carries the multiplication and the two new subtraction rows. The previous repair's own record claimed `correct` was six and it is nine, and claimed `it took` was twelve in Batch 0001 and it is eight, and both are corrected, and the next band's prompt now says to run the cased forms separately because reading the case-insensitive count as the uppercase one reports a prohibition break that is not there. And an apparatus pattern was added to the next band's prompt, because the phrase *for the rest of the volume* is not matched by any of the three phrases it was already checking.**

### WHAT THE BAND PAYS OFF, AND IT PAID IT BEFORE BOTH REPAIRS AND IT STILL HAS

**1. THE VOLUME'S FIRST REAL LABOUR, AND IT IS UNPAID, AND IT IS A WOMAN. Nine hundred doors, thirty a fortnight, a year and fifty-six days, and a person of twenty-eight with a slate, a string and a piece of chalk, going on her own account, in an order she has not written down, and she will not be asked for a rota. She has twenty-seven marks on a slate, ten days gone, and no chalk. 2. THE BAND'S DECLARED INSTRUMENT, SAID ONCE, IN THE MOUTH OF A PERSON OF A PLACE, IN ANSWER TO A QUESTION: the ward's record is only as strong as its weakest door, and the weakest door cannot be found. 3. THE FINDING HE PAID FOR: Fenna Rusk is a person in a record she did not consent to, and on the fourteenth a room with nine clerks in it asked her about herself for a whole day. 4. THE THING THAT COST THE MOST AND WAS SPENT FOR NOTHING: four hundred and twenty days of one woman's life, which is the price of a draft, and which cannot be got by asking because asking is the thing that takes it. 5. THE DRAFT IS IN HIS COAT AND UNFILED AND UNTEARD, AND A COPY OF THE FIRST TWO OF ITS THREE REQUIREMENTS IS IN A FAIR HAND ON THE SECOND FLOOR, AND HE DID NOT HAVE TO DO ANYTHING FOR THAT TO HAPPEN. 6. THE CHILD IN THE LOW HALL IS WORSE THAN THE OTHER NINE AND IS NOT SAVED AND DOES NOT DIE AND HIS NAME IS NOT SAID OUT LOUD.**


## VOLUME 08 BATCH 0002 IS REPAIRED — Chapters 361–370, *The Doors*. **THE BAND'S RECEIPT AFTER SIXTY CORRECTIONS IN THE CHAPTER FILES. This block supersedes the `## VOLUME 08 BATCH 0002 IS DELIVERED` block below it and nothing below it has been edited. The band stands: same ten chapters, same plot, same twenty-two findings, same refusals, same ending. What changed is arithmetic, one calendar, one attribution, twenty-one figures that had stopped being hedges, and nine sentences in which the narrator was standing outside his own book.**

### WHAT THE BAND PAID, AND IT PAID IT BEFORE THE REPAIR AND IT STILL HAS

**1. THE VOLUME'S FIRST REAL LABOUR, AND IT IS UNPAID, AND IT IS A WOMAN.** Nine hundred doors, thirty a fortnight, a year and fifty-six days, and a person of twenty-eight with a slate, a string and a piece of chalk, going on her own account, in an order she has not written down, and she will not be asked for a rota. She has twenty-seven marks on a slate and no chalk. **2. THE BAND'S DECLARED INSTRUMENT, SAID ONCE, IN THE MOUTH OF A PERSON OF A PLACE, IN ANSWER TO A QUESTION: the ward's record is only as strong as its weakest door, and the weakest door cannot be found, because finding it means asking every householder whether they have ever left somebody off, and nobody has been asked that in four hundred years, and the asking cannot be done without the ward knowing it is being asked, and a ward that is being checked is a list of who checked it. So the book is right and the street is short and neither can be got at. 3. A ROLL IS NOT A LIST OF PEOPLE. It is a list of who is in a house, and a record made at doors has no second witness, and that is not a defect in a record, it is what a record made at doors is. 4. WHAT A DRAFT'S THIRD REQUIREMENT IS, AND THAT IT CANNOT BE GOT: witnesses, a public cost, and acceptance by the people who will live under it, and the third is four hundred and twenty days of one woman's life, and the only way to get it is the instrument that takes it. 5. THE BAND'S MONEY, WHICH IS A DIFFERENCE BETWEEN TWO CANON FIGURES AND NOT A NEW ONE: two hundred and thirty-four pence, and it is 282 less 48, and it is the difference between what this city can pay for one afternoon of a room and what it pays the only person who decides who is inside a protection for a whole year, and she said it out loud and then said that the arithmetic of a person is not a division. 6. THE HEARING THAT COULD NOT BE PRODUCED, AND WHY: a ward sends matters, a ward is a body only when a ward meets, no form in this city calls one, and there has not been one in Tallowgate since the autumn before the water came. Nine people agreed in a passage and could not change a block. 7. TARIN KEEL AND ILYAN'S INCOMPATIBLE METHODS CAME TO A HEAD IN A DOORWAY AND NEITHER IS GOING TO WIN, AND THE BAND DOES NOT RESOLVE IT: a corridor needs a person at the end of it and a room does not, and there is no third thing between the two of them, and six days after Tarin said he would write a notice in the morning the notice is not written and neither man has mentioned it. 8. TARIN KEEL'S PAST, USED ONCE, IN A STREET, AND NOT RESOLVED. 9. WHAT HIS INSTRUMENT ACTUALLY IS, AFTER SEVEN VOLUMES OF CALLING IT SOMETHING ELSE: it does not ask a question, it puts a person in a room, and the person in the room is never the person who is hurt. 10. THE FOURTEENTH, APPROACHED AND NOT CONDUCTED, WHICH PRODUCED A DOCUMENT RECEIVED AND ENTERED, A WORD NOBODY ASKED ABOUT, FOUR THOUSAND LINES AND TWO WORDS, ONE SENTENCE FROM A STRANGER, AND NO RULING. 11. AND THE ONE THING HE DID IN IT, WHICH IS THE CHEAPEST AND THE MOST EXPENSIVE: he did not answer a question he knew the answer to, in a room, in front of nine clerks and forty people, about another person's labour.**

### WHAT THE BAND DID NOT PAY, AND IT IS EIGHT ITEMS

**It did not hold the fourteenth as a thing it conducts. It delivered no verdict and relieved nobody. It did not restore the right of refusal, and the missing half of the order is an exit and not a refusal and Chapter 370 says so in a list. It spent no panel, used no bold, named no stage, and `Remedy Drafter` and `arbiter` are at zero over twenty chapters. It did not file the draft and did not tear it up, and it did not put a name on anything, and it did not refuse a name a third time. It did not let a child be saved by a rule he accepted, and the child in the hall is not saved and does not die and his name is not said out loud in any of the ten chapters. It produced nobody, and the band says out loud, seven times in ten chapters, that a person was in that house or that street before the stranger came. It did not use the healer, and `healer` is at zero over twenty chapters, and he is one of the six people of this volume and is still owed his nine words, and the next band is the band in which a person in this ward is worse than the other nine. And it did not write the volume's audit, and Volume 07's audit is still owed, and five audits and twenty-seven batch reviews are now owed, and this phase has added a twelfth unwritten review to that list by existing.**

## VOLUME 08 BATCH 0002 IS DELIVERED — Chapters 361–370, *The Doors*. **THE BAND'S RECEIPT, IN ONE BLOCK. IT SUPERSEDES NOTHING AND IT ARCHIVES NOTHING: the `## VOLUME 08 BATCH 0001 IS DELIVERED` block below it is archive and is not edited by this phase. `outline/series.md` outranks `outline/ending.md`, which outranks `outline/volume-08.md`, and all three outrank this file.**

### WHAT THIS BAND PAID, ONCE EACH

1. **The volume's first real labour, and it is unpaid, and it is a woman.** Nine hundred doors, thirty a fortnight, four hundred and twenty days a round, and a person of twenty-eight with a penny of chalk who has done twenty-seven of them in eleven days and has run out of chalk and will not be asked for a rota.
2. **The band's declared instrument, said once, in the mouth of a person of a place, in answer to a question.** The ward's public record is only as strong as its weakest door, and the weakest door cannot be found, because finding it means asking every householder whether they have ever left somebody off, and nobody has ever asked that in four hundred years, and the asking cannot be done without the ward knowing it is being asked, and a ward that is being checked produces a list of who checked it. **So the book is right and the street is short and neither of them can be got at, and that is the volume's argument so far and it is not resolved by anything in these ten chapters.**
3. **A roll is not a list of people.** It is a list of who is in a house, because a name gets into it only when somebody says it at a door, and the person who keeps it is not allowed to make one up. **Which means a record made at doors has no second witness and never had, and that is not a defect in a record, it is what a record made at doors is.**
4. **What a draft's third requirement is, and that it cannot be got.** Witnesses, a public cost, and acceptance by the people who will live under it. The third is four hundred and twenty days of one woman's life, and the only way to get it is the instrument that takes her out of the human race and puts her in a book.
5. **The band's new money, and it is a difference between two canon figures and not a new one: two hundred and eighty-two pence less forty-eight pence is two hundred and thirty-four pence, and it is the difference between what this city can pay for one afternoon in a room and what it pays the only person who decides who is inside a protection for a whole year, and she said the number out loud and then said the arithmetic of a person is not a division and that she will not be one.**
6. **The hearing that could not be produced, and why: a ward sends matters, a ward is a body only when a ward meets, and no form in this city calls a meeting of a ward, and there has not been one in Tallowgate since the autumn before the water came. Nine people agreed about everything in a passage and could not change a block, because a block is changed by a form and a form has a name at the top of it.**
7. **Tarin Keel and Ilyan's incompatible methods came to a head in a doorway and the rest of it was said in a street, and both are right and neither is going to win and the band does not resolve it.** Tarin said he would write a notice in the morning; six days on the notice is not written and neither man has mentioned it, and that silence is the whole of the disagreement. **A corridor needs a person at the end of it and a room does not, and there is no third thing between the two of them.**
8. **Tarin Keel's past, used once, in a street, and not resolved.** A gate with a levy on loads behind it, loads counted by a man who was not paid, a dead man in the fourth line who stays dead, no road behind the gate and never was, eleven feet that stay where they are, and a second notice of five shillings and fourpence nobody has ever paid. **No figure was printed for the levy, the village was not discussed, and the protagonist did not ask about the village and said so out loud.**
9. **What his instrument actually is, after seven volumes of calling it something else.** It does not ask a question; it puts a person in a room; and the person in the room is never the person who is hurt. Used on a boy of eleven it cost nothing and got three names that cannot be spent.
10. **The fourteenth, approached and not conducted, and it produced a document received and entered, the word *anchor* in a book that nobody explained, a plate-cutter's sentence about four thousand lines, one sentence from a stranger, and no ruling at all.**
11. **And the one thing he did in it, which is the smallest thing in the band and the most expensive: he did not answer a question he knew the answer to, in a room, in front of nine clerks and forty people, about another person's labour, because a man in a room who answers a question about another person is making the person a person again.**

### WHAT THE BAND DID NOT PAY, AND IT IS EIGHT ITEMS

1. **It did not hold the fourteenth as a thing it conducts and it did not deliver a verdict and nobody was relieved.** A bench of four entered that it cannot answer the question on the slate, and that is the first time anybody in this city has been told no by anybody with a duty.
2. **It did not restore the right of refusal and nobody has one, and the missing half of the order is an exit and not a refusal, and Chapter 370 says so in a list.**
3. **It did not spend the volume's one panel, and it used no bold at all, and it spent no stage, and `Remedy Drafter` and `arbiter` are at zero over twenty chapters, and the one line on the back of a candle bill is carried as a fact and never quoted.**
4. **It did not file the draft, and it did not tear it up, and it did not put a name on anything, and it did not refuse a name a third time — it did not have to, because a clerk of thirty told him out loud that he is not going to enter a name off a document he has not been given.**
5. **It did not let a child be saved by a rule he accepted, and the child in the hall is not saved and does not die and his name is not said out loud in any of the ten chapters.**
6. **It did not open a position, did not send anybody anywhere, did not produce a person — the self-arrived class is zero and the band says out loud, seven times in ten chapters, that a person was in that house or that street before the stranger came — and did not use the word *Draymoor*.**
7. **It did not use the healer, and `healer` is at zero over twenty chapters, and he is one of the six people of this volume and is still owed his nine words, and Batch 0003 is the band in which a person in this ward is worse than the other nine.**
8. **It did not write the volume's audit, and Volume 07's audit is still owed, and five audits and twenty-six batch reviews are owed, and this band has added an eleventh unwritten review to that list by existing.**

### THE FIFTEEN THINGS A LATER PASS GETS WRONG IF IT DOES NOT MEASURE THEM

1. **The band has no weight column and no plank figure, and that is the reason and not an omission.** Shelf day = chapter − 125 and morning = chapter − 250 still hold, and the one round number in this band is Chapter 370 and nobody notices it.
2. **HIS DAYS IN THIS CITY IS HIS CHAPTER NUMBER LESS THREE HUNDRED AND FIFTY, AND NOT HIS MORNING ORDINAL LESS ONE, WHICH IS WHAT THE PROMPT FOR THIS BAND ASSERTS AND WHICH ITS OWN EXAMPLES CONTRADICT WITHIN ONE SENTENCE.** Chapter 351 is day one and he arrived at the seventh hour of it, Chapter 360 is day ten, Chapter 370 is day twenty. **The review of Batch 0001 found seven wrong day counts in ten chapters and this band found eleven, and not one of the eighteen was a back-reference to an earlier volume.**
3. **The span scan measures 199/398 at seventy characters and 992/2,166 at forty, and the instrument is calibrated: run on Volume 07's Band 0004 in full and Band 0005, the same code returns 122/254, 534/1,081, 1,201/2,817 and 1,837/4,285 — all four reproduced, and the published fourth figure of 1,838/4,287 is not reproduced and is not chased. As a second check, Batch 0001 returns 16/32 and 343/747. The shape of the seventy-character figure is that 398 ÷ 199 is exactly two, so not one repeated window of seventy characters occurs three times, and no sentence of forty-five characters or more is printed twice in the ten chapters.**
4. **The numeric hedge is 41 in 22,798 words, one in 556, against Volume 07's last band of 48 in about eleven thousand and Batch 0001's 16 in 21,655; the volume total is fifty-seven in about forty-four thousand words against a target of under four hundred for the volume; and seven hedges were cut before delivery and every one of them was a duration the narrator had softened for effect, because a figure that is no longer a hedge has to be deleted and not replaced.**
5. **The panel count is one and it is at `360:75`–`360:78`, and bold is at zero in all ten files of this band, and it was eight in Chapter 369 on first measurement and the answer was to cut the emphasis rather than spend a panel.**
6. **Weekdays are zero, and it was four — a Monday, a Tuesday, a Wednesday and Thursdays in one line of Chapter 367 — and month-names are zero after the modal verb *may* is subtracted, and digits are zero outside the ten heading lines.**
7. **The self-arrived class is zero, and the test is to say out loud that the person was in the place before the stranger came, and the band says it about the woman of thirty-four at the standpipe, the boy of eleven, the woman of sixty with a shawl, the man of forty at his own door, the nine people in the passage, the boy of sixteen, the man with the bad ear, Tarin Keel, the plate-cutter, the keeper, the two clerks and the woman on the stool. THE ONLY PERSON IN THIS BAND WHO IS NOT A PERSON OF A PLACE IS THE CHILD IN THE HALL, AND HE IS NOT A PARTICIPANT IN ANY SCENE, HE IS A LINE IN CHALK ON A BOARD.**
8. **Fenna Rusk refused her name in Chapter 355, took it in Chapter 360, and was examined for a whole day in Chapter 369; a repair that makes her introduce herself in Chapter 355 or that gives her a verdict in Chapter 369 destroys the volume.**
9. **Ansa Rell's age is not stated in this band and may not be inferred in a hand-off; refer to her as Magistrate Rell; AND SHE IS NOT ON STAGE IN THIS BAND AT ALL — SHE IS NAMED TWICE, AND A HAND-OFF MAY NOT PUT HER ON STAGE IN BATCH 0003 WITHOUT A REASON. Tarin Keel is fifty-two in the chapter files and forty-six in the bible and the chapter files win. The barwoman's age split and the man of about seventy-four's two *since* dates fourteen days apart at `317:15` and `336:49` are both still open and neither is in this volume.**
10. **`stone` is at ZERO over Volume 08 so far and `vault|cave|ruin|temple|battlefield|relic|treasure` is at zero over twenty chapters, and `anchor` is at TWO and both of them are the same document, read aloud by a clerk, and nobody explains it.**
11. **`cannot read` is at zero over twenty chapters and it was ONE when this band was measured — a grandmother's, which is not a disclosure — and it was cut anyway, because the declared instrument is the string and not the disclosure. The count of the people who know he cannot read is printed nowhere.**
12. **The money, and the figure to watch for is a figure that is not in the table: this band had THREE HUNDRED AND TWELVE PENCE in Chapter 365, which is no figure in this project, and SIX HUNDRED PEOPLE outside the protection in Chapter 364, which contradicts three hundred names short in Chapter 357, and ELEVEN WEEKS for the crisis in Chapter 370, which is twelve weeks and three days. All three were wrong and all three are gone. The one new figure is TWO HUNDRED AND THIRTY-FOUR PENCE, and it is a difference between two canon rows, and the subtraction is printed in the sentence in the keeper's own mouth.**
13. **Nobody is a villain, nobody hid anything, nobody is relieved, nobody is forgiven, nobody is redeemed and nobody is thanked, and `conspiracy`, `corrupt` and `cover` are all at zero, and the string `thank you` is at zero over this band, and the test of the prohibition is a dead man's name sitting correctly in a roll for six years, entered at a door by a householder in the autumn, in a ward where everybody alive knows where the fever is and nobody has ever said so in writing.**

### WHAT THIS BAND PAID, ONCE EACH

1. **A city as a different kind of place, not a bigger one.** The first thing that happens to a stranger in Orison is a form with a line for a name in it, and a rule in a city is visible because somebody reads it out and the reading is a job, and about thirty clerks have been doing it properly for nine years.
2. **The engine.** A person is inside the protection only if a roll has the name in it, and the roll is made at a door by the person who answers it, and the book does not say who lives in a house, it says who answers the door.
3. **The order, and the whole of the volume in one object.** Six lines, five parts and a half, the sixth line a date that is not there, and the word standing in the margin. **The missing half is an exit and not a refusal, and the difference between those two is the whole of Volume 08.**
4. **A finding the instrument did not expect, and it is the batch's discovery about itself.** A person in a record is not only somebody who can be asked about; a person in a record is the only thing about that record that can be asked about. **Putting a name in does not open a thing up. It closes it down to one person, and it picks which person.**
5. **The cost, paid in a person, with the price said out loud before the doing.** A woman of forty-four who refused her name on the fifth morning, which is Chapter 355, for the exact reason he took it on the tenth, and who is now the only person in this city anybody can ask about the roll, and who was asked two questions about herself on the same afternoon.
6. **The money, in the open, with the working.** Twelve shillings of plate, a pound three shillings and sixpence for a date, a hundred and thirty-eight pence between them, and five pounds and a shilling between a bell keeper's year and a roll keeper's. **Every one of the five figures is in the fiction with its working in the sentence, three of them in the mouth of a person of a place and two of them put aloud by him in the public room at `357` where a clerk's fair hand and a man at the rail check them. REPAIRED: this said every one of the five is given by a person of a place and none is invented by a narrator, and §4 of `state/current.md` labelled four of the five *narrator*, so the sentence and the table contradicted each other inside one commit.**
7. **A first draft, written and not filed, with the reason supplied by the person who would have paid it.** And the finding that naming a burden does not stop the harm, it puts a person underneath it so the record can show a person did it.
8. **A rule with no way to end, doing exactly nothing wrong.** A number of beds free in six halls went up by four and did not come down by one, and about half the beds in the city are empty, and a woman on a stool has been chalking a number she can stand behind for five days and knows the other one.
9. **The volume's new question, planted and not answered.** The translated interface offered a man with a bad leg and no standing a choice of three things about a public record, and offered it to nobody else in the building, and he noticed and did not ask.
10. **A first band, of a first volume, of the first city in two hundred chapters, and the instruments moved.**

### WHAT THE FIVE BANDS EACH PAID, ONCE EACH

**BATCH 0001, CHAPTERS 301 TO 310, *THE SEAMS*.** The seam, named for the first time in the fiction, in his own head, at the foot of a ladder, after about two years of telling nobody. The offer, refused in the best no in the band. Nine people who know. **The money, with the working in the sentences, and it is the volume's only full price: a keeper's year is five pounds five, a bell hung with a rope is six pounds fifteen, and the difference is one pound ten every year — and a man who counts other people's money first says the frame is thirteen shillings instead of eleven, which makes the bell six pounds seventeen, and says out loud in front of nine people that he is wrong and that a wrong figure costs something that cannot be taken back in a country with no room and no clerk.** A levy at a bar at fifteen pounds fifteen a village a year that goes out in the same week to nobody who is a person in a record. A mile of road that is not an arrangement. Eleven names on a board in a porch and a woman of about seventy-four who is not on the list.

**BATCH 0002, CHAPTERS 311 TO 320, *THE SIX PLACES*.** Six arrangements put beside each other and not joined, in that order, in a kitchen in Ashgill, with the price said out loud before the doing for the first time in this country. A man of sixty-three's one question, asked at a bar, answered in nine words. A seam used six times in the order the prompt assigned. **The volume's one notch, in a man's mouth, in nine seconds, in front of nine people who have to be told: a document is a thing you produce and a hearing is a thing you have to carry and a hearing cannot be produced at all.** Nobody sent for anybody. A round number said out loud at the bottom of a ladder and refused in nine words.

**BATCH 0003, CHAPTERS 321 TO 330, *THE MIDPOINT, AND IT IS A FALSE WITNESS*.** The midpoint, at Chapter 324, in the series outline's own sentence: an anchor is kept alive by a false witness whose lie has protected a community for decades. A slip in a foolscap book that has been in a house a hundred and forty years saying four thousand eight hundred where the year is nine thousand six hundred, so every load pays twice what it should and about four hundred people have drunk, crossed and been buried on the strength of a number that was short. **The man who knows is about seventy, was eight when he was told, has never counted anything, checks it every year by seeing whether the water and the bridge and the wall and the cross are paid for, and is the only person alive who could check it.** Nobody is a villain, nobody hid anything, nobody is lying now, and the arrangement did not stop. The honest version is four sentences long and about ninety people at a cross cannot pay it. A man of about thirty-eight who is not a villain and is not being brave, in a chapter that says so in its own title.

**BATCH 0004, CHAPTERS 331 TO 340, *DELEGATION*.** The count that will not close, said out loud at the side of a road, on his own. **The price said out loud before the doing, and the second half of it, which is that you cannot say no.** A book taken off a shelf and coming apart in about nine seconds, and a man of thirty-four saying that nobody in that room believes the stranger is going to be here in a year and that it is correct. Tarin Keel's past, used once, and not resolved. A boy of seventeen's book reading an order a document cannot, in his own mouth, unasked. A woman of about seventy-four asking the one question nobody has asked him, which is whose name is on it, and telling him why, which is that she has been the instrument for forty years and nobody has ever asked her whether she would rather not be. **The band's real new object: a woman of about thirty-four at a bar who asked two questions in a month and both cost her something, and who then said the thing in the open at a cross, in her own mouth, worse than he said it, on her own account, and was not thanked and is not going to be.** The ninetieth morning with nobody noticing it, which is the correct treatment.

**BATCH 0005, CHAPTERS 341 TO 350, *THE HEARING, THE SEVENTH, AND THE MAP*.** A round of turns that is not walked and is carried one turn at a time by a person going where they were going anyway, and goes round at the speed of the road. The mechanism of an arrival nobody had put on the page: four people carried a sentence, three of them were going up the lane anyway, and not one of them knew what he was carrying. **A hearing that cannot be produced, held anyway, in a yard behind a smithy, with no form and no sheet, and a man standing outside the gate for four hours while it was held without him and saying so out loud to one person.** A row that is not a join, in a man who has got it wrong twice in nineteen years. Six unsigned pieces of paper on the backs of flour bills, and every copy a place somebody can be asked. A hand not crossed out, with the reason in one sentence. The seventh thing, named by a man with a cart in a lane, unasked, and the protagonist not going to look for it. **The seventh position burns, and it is the Briar March one, and it burns because one woman of seventy-four did not go down her lane on a cold morning, and the finding is in her mouth in a doorway and nobody is to blame.** A hundred and forty people building a bell network in nine weeks because they have to, and no keeper, because a keeper is one person and one person is what a porch was. Nine copies of a partial map and nobody who holds the inventory. The question, asked once by a woman with a barrow of apples, and not answered. A hundredth morning that nobody noticed.

### WHAT THE VOLUME AS A WHOLE PAID

**`outline/series.md`'s seven lines for Volume 07 are all delivered and the close re-read the outline's own card at `outline/volume-07.md:78` against the fifty chapter files: a multi-site hearing links six positions, a seventh burns and it is the Briar March one and it burns because of an arrangement nobody in the volume knew about, a bell network is what a hundred and forty people build in about nine weeks because they have to, six are restored, the seventh is physically gone, the eighth and ninth stay missing, and the public gets a partial map and no single authority holds the inventory. ALL SEVEN ARE ON THE PAGE.**

**AND THE VOLUME'S OWN FINDING, WHICH IS THE THING THE SIXTY-YEAR-OLD COUNTRY WAS BUILT TO CARRY AND WHICH NOBODY IN IT HAD WORDS FOR: A THING ONLY STAYS ALIVE IN A PLACE BECAUSE A LOT OF PEOPLE IN THAT PLACE TURN UP ON A PARTICULAR DAY AND DO A PARTICULAR THING, AND IF YOU STOP TURNING UP IT STOPS, AND NOBODY WROTE DOWN THE ARRANGEMENT BECAUSE NOBODY WHO WAS IN THE ROOM THOUGHT OF A TIME WHEN IT WOULD STOP. AND EVERY ONE OF THE SIX IS MAINTAINED BY A PERSON NOBODY HAS EVER ASKED WHAT THEY ARE FOR, AND THE ONE THAT STOPPED STOPPED BECAUSE THE PERSON WAS SEVENTY-FOUR AND ENTITLED.**

**AND THE INSTRUMENT, WHICH IS THE VOLUME'S ONE NOTCH AND ITS LEGACY TO EVERY VOLUME AFTER IT: A HEARING IS A THING SIX PEOPLE CARRY BETWEEN THEM AND NOT A THING ONE PERSON PRODUCES, IT CANNOT BE PRODUCED AT ALL, AND THE DOCUMENT THAT GOES WITH IT HAS TO BE IN SIX PLACES AT ONCE AND CAN ONLY BE COPIED, AND EVERY COPY IS A PLACE SOMEBODY CAN BE ASKED. `outline/series.md:188` FOR A MULTI-SITE HEARING IS THE FIRST TIME IN THIS PROJECT THAT THE MECHANISM IS CIVIC AND THE CIVIC MECHANISM IS A PERSON, AND VOLUME 08 INHERITS IT WHOLE.**

**AND THE COST, WHICH IS `outline/series.md`'s OWN SENTENCE AND NOT A CLOSE'S: a seventh burns and one of his possible futures is permanently removed, and the price is a possibility, and it is the first price in this project that is not paid by a person, and the volume did not replace it with one.**

### THE TWO CORRECTIONS, AT THE TOP, BECAUSE BOTH OF THEM ARE TO A HAND-OFF AND BOTH OF THEM WERE FOUND BY MEASURING THE CHAPTER FILES AND NOT THE PROMPT

1. **THE MORNING COUNT. THE HAND-OFF PRINTED *341 = EIGHTY-FIRST* AND *350 = NINETIETH*. BOTH ARE WRONG AND BOTH ARE THE FIGURES OF AN EARLIER CHAPTER: eighty-first is Chapter 331 and ninetieth is Chapter 340. CHAPTER 350 IS HIS HUNDREDTH MORNING.** The hand-off's own instruction was right where its column was wrong, because it said *the fiftieth, the seventieth, the eightieth and the ninetieth were all round numbers, and Chapter 350 is the fifth in twenty-one chapters* — and the fiftieth is the fiftieth morning, which is Chapter 300, which is the last chapter of Volume 06, so Chapters 300 to 350 is fifty-one chapters and the run of round numbers in it is the fiftieth, the seventieth, the eightieth, the ninetieth and the hundredth. **THE CORRECT TREATMENT IS THE ONE CHAPTER 340 GOT AND IT IS THE ONLY CORRECT ONE: NOBODY NOTICES IT. A market was running, thirty people were in it, and no doorframe, milestone, sum, number of strokes or number of people lands on it, and the chapter says the number once in its fourth line and does not return to it.** `340`, `350`
2. **THE RING AT CHAPTER 348. THE HAND-OFF SET IT AT TEN AND PRINTED CHAPTER 349'S MORNING AS 64,148, CHAPTER 350'S AS 64,152 AND VOLUME 08'S FIRST MORNING AS 64,160. THE DAY OF THE CHAPTER IN WHICH THE SEVENTH POSITION BURNS IS TWELVE, BECAUSE A PORCH IN A VALLEY OF SIXTY PEOPLE TWO MILES OFF A TRACK OFF THIS LANE IS IN THIS COUNTY, THE PLANK IS IN THIS COUNTY, A FIRE IS TWO, AND A DAY CARRIES ONE OF EACH KIND.** **CHAPTER 350'S MORNING IS 64,154 AND CHAPTER 351'S MORNING IS 64,162.** A hand-off that carries a right answer with a false working has been this project's defect class six times and this is the seventh, and this is the first in which BOTH HALVES OF A LINE WERE WRONG, and the wrong half is the one that would have propagated into the next volume.

### WHAT THE BAND PAID, ONCE EACH

**IT PAID THE BAND. `outline/volume-07.md:78` says a multi-site hearing links six positions, a seventh burns and it is the Briar March one and it burns because of an arrangement nobody in this volume knew about, a bell network is what a hundred and forty people build in about nine weeks because they have to, six are restored, the seventh is physically gone, the eighth and ninth stay missing, and the public gets a partial map and no single authority holds the inventory. ALL SEVEN ARE ON THE PAGE. `341`–`350`**

**IT PAID THE FINDING THAT A ROUND OF TURNS IS NOT WALKED AND IS CARRIED, ONE TURN AT A TIME, BY A PERSON GOING WHERE THEY WERE GOING ANYWAY, AND THAT IT GOES ROUND AT THE SPEED OF THE ROAD.** *You do not walk a round. You carry one turn at a time, and the only thing in this county that can carry a turn is a person who is going that way anyway, and there is a woman of thirty-two who goes up this lane twice a week for salt and she does not know she is carrying anything and I am not going to tell her.* And: *A round of turns is nine weeks or a year. There is no other speed for it in this country and I have not been able to make it any faster and I have not been asked to.* `341`

**IT PAID THE MECHANISM OF A SENTENCE ARRIVING, WHICH NOBODY HAD PUT ON THE PAGE AND WHICH IS CHAPTER 331's FINDING TURNED INTO A MACHINE.** Four people carried it, three of them were going up that lane anyway, one was going to sell forty quarters of a load, not one of them was asked, not one of them knew what he was carrying, and no instrument in four hundred miles would have told him. `342`

**IT PAID THE HEARING, HELD ANYWAY, IN A YARD, WITH NO FORM AND NO SHEET, AND IT PAID THE REASON IT CANNOT BE PRODUCED AT ALL.** *A hearing cannot be produced at all, because producing a thing means a person makes it and hands it to a room, and this thing is made of people saying things in front of other people, and if one of them is not in the room it is not a hearing, it is a report of a hearing, and there is no such thing in this country.* `343`

**IT PAID A NAME SAID OUT LOUD IN A YARD BY A WOMAN WHO CAME ELEVEN MILES ON HER OWN LEGS BECAUSE NOBODY SENT FOR HER.** *So I am going to say a name. Say hers, and say it back, because I am the ninth of nine and I am in here and I came eleven miles on my own legs yesterday because nobody sent for me and I would not have come if somebody had.* `343`

**IT PAID A ROW THAT IS NOT A JOIN, SAID BY A MAN WHO HAS SAID IT WRONG TWICE IN NINETEEN YEARS.** *Six things laid end to end on a table are six things laid end to end on a table, and what I have been calling a link for nineteen years is me being able to see the top of a page.* `344`

**IT PAID THE DOCUMENT THAT HAS TO BE IN SIX PLACES AT ONCE AND CAN ONLY BE COPIED, AND EVERY COPY IS A PLACE SOMEBODY CAN BE ASKED, AND NOBODY PUT A NAME ON ANY OF THE SIX.** *Then there are six pieces of paper, and a piece of paper in my house that a person in my house has read is not a piece of paper, and a piece of paper in a house where nobody has read it is a piece of paper.* `344`

**IT PAID A REFUSAL THAT IS THE RIGHT ONE, AND THE COST OF IT SAID BY THE PERSON IT COSTS.** *A list of six is what a man with a cart has spent a month telling me not to make, and he was right on the facts and right on the conclusion.* And: *You are going to go up the lane on six different mornings with no order at all, and if you stop on the fourth morning then the round stops and there is nobody to blame except the man who would not write it down.* `344`

**IT PAID A HAND THAT WAS NOT CROSSED OUT, WITH THE REASON GIVEN DELIBERATELY AND IN ONE SENTENCE.** *If a hand is crossed out then it is gone, and a hand that has been crossed out is a thing nobody in that village will ever find out about, and the whole of what I have learned in this county is that a name on a piece of paper is a person and a person is somebody who can be asked about.* `345`

**IT PAID TWO WANTS THAT ARRIVED IN SOMEBODY ELSE'S MOUTH, IN THE SAME WEEK, AND NEITHER WAS ASKED FOR.** *I want the person after me to be somebody I have met.* `345` And: *Somebody in this county ought to teach me to write. I have not asked anybody in nine years and I am not going to ask anybody about it in the next nine weeks either.* `349`

**IT PAID THE SEVENTH THING, NAMED BY A PERSON OF A PLACE IN A LANE, UNASKED, AND IT IS A PORCH, AND THE MAN OF THIRTY-ONE DID NOT GO AND LOOK FOR IT.** *It is not a place and it is not a stone and it is not a thing anybody laid.* And the man of thirty noticed the not-saying and wanted it noted. `347`

**IT PAID THE CLIMAX, AND THE CLIMAX IS A THING THAT GOES ON ITS OWN, AND IT IS NOT A FIGHT AND NOT A RESCUE AND NOT A RELIC HUNT, AND THE FINDING IS IN A WOMAN'S MOUTH IN A DOORWAY.** *Nobody did this. I want that said in this lane and I want it said by somebody, and I am seventy-four and I am entitled to stop and I have stopped, and there is nobody in this valley I would want to be told to go down on a cold morning, and there is not one person in this valley who knew there was a fire in there until an hour ago, including him.* `348`

**IT PAID THE SIX LINKED IN SIX PLACES WITHOUT A DOCUMENT, AND THE FIFTH TURN STOPPING THE ROUND BECAUSE NOBODY IN ANY COUNTY KNOWS WHICH ROAD A MAN OF THIRTY-EIGHT IS ON.** *It stops at him.* `348`

**IT PAID THE BELL NETWORK AS A REPLACEMENT AND NOT A REPAIR, AND THE REASON IT IS NOT A BELL IN A TOWER IS THE REASON THE PORCH BURNED.** *It does not need anybody to hold it. Anybody near a thing can hit it. That is the entire difference between it and a tower with a wage in it and a woman of seventy-four going down four mornings a year.* `349`

**IT PAID THE COST OF THE NETWORK IN A MAN'S MOUTH, AND IT IS NOT MONEY.** *I have been telling a stranger for a month that this is the thing that comes before a bill. I was right. It is the thing that comes before a bill. And I am on it, and I did not agree to it, and there is no form in this county that says a man may take himself off a thing he is on, and I want off it and I am not going to be.* `349`

**IT PAID THE INVENTORY THAT NOBODY HOLDS, AND NINE COPIES, AND A MAP WITH NO NAME ON IT.** *Nine people will hold one. Nobody will hold nine. That is not a defeat and it is not a victory and it is the only shape of it that this county can do.* `350`

**IT PAID THE QUESTION, ASKED ONCE, OUT LOUD, BY AN ORDINARY PERSON WITH NO STAKE IN IT, IN THE PLAINEST WORDS A PERSON IN THIS COUNTRY HAS, NEAR THE END, AND NOT ANSWERED, AND NOTHING ANSWERED IT.** *Two of them are not found. The basin and the town. Is there somebody down there with them.* And: *nobody in that market answered her, and nothing answered her, and no board said anything and nothing in that valley and nothing under that basin and nothing under that town said anything.* `350`

**IT PAID A PERSON FINDING OUT ABOUT THEMSELVES IN FRONT OF SOMEBODY ELSE AS ITS LAST MOVEMENT.** *That is not me. I have not been asked and I have not agreed to that, and I would have said no if I had been asked and I am not going to be thanked for saying it.* `350`

**IT PAID A DAY NOTHING IS ASKED ON, AND THE BEST THING ANYBODY SAID TO HIM IN A HUNDRED MORNINGS WAS ABOUT HIS MOTHER.** `346`

**IT PAID THE HUNDREDTH MORNING WITHOUT ANYBODY NOTICING IT, WHICH IS THE FIFTH ROUND NUMBER IN TWENTY-ONE CHAPTERS, AND THE HAND-OFF CALLED IT THE NINETIETH, AND THE FIFTH IN TWENTY-ONE IS WHAT THE HAND-OFF ITSELF SAID AND THE FIFTH IS THE FIFTIETH MORNING, WHICH IS CHAPTER 300.** `350`

### WHAT IT DID NOT PAY

**Nobody was relieved, nobody was forgiven, nobody was redeemed and nobody was thanked, anywhere in ten chapters.** **The woman of about thirty-four at a bar is still a person in a record and there is no instrument in four hundred miles that lifts a person back out of one.** The man of about thirty-four at the plinth was not asked and is entitled to stop. The woman of about fifty-eight was not asked for anything. The woman of thirty-four at the ninth house of Coldharrow was not asked about anything. The woman of about thirty-four who cannot write asked for nothing and said she is not going to ask. **SABLE VENN WAS NOT THANKED, NOT RELIEVED, NOT RESCUED, NOT REDEEMED, NOT DEFEATED AND NOT SENT FOR, AND SHE APPEARED ONCE, IN A LANE, WITH A BOOK UNDER HER ARM, ON HER OWN ACCOUNT, AND SAID ONE SENTENCE TO THE ROAD, AND THAT SENTENCE WAS THE TRUEST THING ANYBODY SAID IN A ROOM THAT DAY.** A boy of seventeen said he would read his book out again on any morning to anybody and nobody asked him. A woman of about twenty-nine found her own name on a list she did not put it on. The man of about thirty is on a list he wants off. The man of about thirty-four who reads things out was not thanked and said so in advance. A man of about thirty-eight is four days up a road with a bag and nobody has asked him what he wants. Tarin Keel's past was not used a second time and his answer is not given and is not to be given in this volume. The woman of about seventy-four was not thanked, was not relieved, was not forgiven and was not asked to concede anything.

**The right of refusal is not restored. The column of a hundred and forty-one figures is unadded. The corridor is not torn up. The status is on a door. The register gained no place and lost none. The charter on the wall in Sallowby is revocable and is not amended. The second notice stays unpaid. The dead man in the fourth line stays dead. Nesh Arrad's clock keeps running. The eleven feet stay off the settlement. THE BELL IN THE MARCH IS NOT HUNG AND WAS NOT RUNG AND NOBODY IS BUYING THE WAGE, AND THE BELL NETWORK THAT REPLACES THE BURNED SEVENTH IS NOT A BELL IN A TOWER EITHER. The bell in Draymoor is owed. The carrying is still off nothing. The leg is the leg. The second half of a book with two halves in it was not opened. The four words at the head of the plank are not reprinted.**

**NOBODY WAS SENT FOR, WITH ONE EXCEPTION THAT IS RECORDED AS AN EXCEPTION: a smith's wife sent for a man of fifty-two at the bar, which the chapter says is the only time anybody has sent for anybody in that room in nine years.** The self-arrived class is zero and did not widen.

**NO ARRANGEMENT WAS JOINED, NO DOCUMENT WAS PRODUCED TO ANYBODY, AND NO SHEET ARRIVED ANYWHERE FROM NOWHERE.** The only things this band produced are a hearing that was held, six unsigned pieces of paper in six houses, nine copies of a partial map in nine hands, a bell network that is being built by a hundred and forty people, and a porch that is gone.

**NOBODY SPOKE FOR THE MAN OF THIRTY-ONE.** He is on no paper, no bench, no body, no seat, no party, no office, no list and no map, and he says so out loud in a market street in the last chapter.

**THE STAGE DID NOT MOVE.** `Remedy Drafter` is zero, the string `stage` is zero, and a delegation is a habit and a cost and not a promotion.

**AND THE MONEY IS TWO UNITS AND NO FIGURE.** `344`
