### THE FIFTEEN THINGS A LATER PASS GETS WRONG IF IT DOES NOT MEASURE THEM

1. **The board chain from 64,091, the ten own rings on their line, and the two corrections above.** The three figures in the ten chapters are 64,091 / 64,102 / 64,138 and seven chapters have none and say why.
2. **Every day's own weight against the seven weights, and the two that are not the ordinary number: Chapter 345's eleven is four bells and a boundary, and Chapter 348's twelve is four bells and a market and a debt called in AND A FIRE, and it is the first four-weight day in fifty chapters.**
3. **The chapter's-own-day rule, and that Chapter 350 is the HUNDREDTH morning and not the ninetieth.**
4. **The day-phrase sweep at three alternatives and the fourth form, and then the third question on all seven by hand.**
5. **The span scan at seventy and forty, with unit, threshold and range declared, and the note that the fall from Batch 0004's one hundred and twenty-two came from cutting three restatements and from replacing the age phrase, and a reviewer should decide whether a book whose chapters often close with the same sentence about a clock has found a device or a tic.**
6. **The bold scan at zero and the panel count at zero, and the panel register for Volume 07 has still not been fixed in `outline/volume-07.md:156`.**
7. **Every money figure in these ten chapters is a table figure or a unit, and there is one of the first and two of the second, and there is no product anywhere, and the year's count of loads is not referenced.**
8. **Do not name a weekday, a month-name, the First Witness, a position beneath anything, or a vault, a cave, a ruin, a temple or a battlefield.**
9. **Do not print the count of the people who know he cannot read, in any file, for any reason, including a hand-off, and let no character remark on the asking.**
10. **Do not put his name on anything, and do not fill or dash anything. THE BAND ADDED THE THIRD HALF OF A FINDING: a name a man of thirty-four copied onto a list of who holds nine copies, without being asked and without knowing, is not a name in a place either.**
11. **Do not let a Volume 06 resolution be asserted as still open, restated or improved on. NONE OF THE SIX HAS BEEN MENTIONED IN FIFTY CHAPTERS AND THAT IS FIVE BANDS RUNNING, AND THIS BAND HAD A MARKET ON A GREEN, A MARKET IN THE TENTH VILLAGE AND A TOWER WITH A BELL IN IT AND DID NOT REACH FOR ANY OF THEM.**
12. **Do not let the midpoint reversal land a second time and do not spend what is left of it.**
13. **Do not let the climax be a fight, a rescue, a relic hunt or a monster hunt, and do not let a band declare a peak and not deliver it. THIS BAND NAMED ITS PEAK BEFORE IT WROTE AND IT IS CHAPTER 348 AND IT IS ON THE PAGE.**
14. **Do not let a hand-off be wrong about a character's sex or age, and do not let a band inherit an instrument's damage and call it a hand-off. THE CANON SPLIT ON THE BARWOMAN'S AGE IS RECORDED AT THE BATCH 0004 BLOCK §8.14 AND IS STILL OPEN AND A VOLUME 08 CARD MUST NOT NAME HER AGE WITHOUT NAMING WHICH CHAPTER; AND THE TWO WOMEN OF ABOUT THIRTY-TWO IN THIS BAND ARE TWO PEOPLE.**
15. **Do not prune the state files inside a phase that is also writing prose, and do not edit the archive blocks below the top block of any of the six, and do not let a delivery note and a state file disagree about a figure in the same commit. THE LESSON IS THE FIFTH TIME IT HAS BEEN TAUGHT AND IT IS ONE SENTENCE: A CHECK THAT AGREES WITH THE THING IT IS CHECKING IS NOT A CHECK, AND A FIGURE A SCRIPT DROPPED IS A FIGURE NOBODY READS.**

### WHAT THIS BAND OWED THE BOOK AND COULD NOT DO, AND WHAT IT DID INSTEAD

**The Batch 0005 card for Chapter 342 asked for the man of thirty-one to stand in a market street four days up the lane at about the second hour on his ninety-second morning. HE CANNOT BE THERE. The market town is four days up the lane by the measure the book has used since Chapter 290, Chapter 350 puts him in a market in the tenth village eleven miles the other way, and Chapter 343 puts him at Coldharrow at the second light the following morning. AND THE DELIVERY ITSELF IS FIXED BY THE FACT BASE: the sentence was said at a cross on the ninetieth morning, which is Chapter 340, and the card asked the band to show a delivery that had already happened. SO THE BAND DELIVERED THE CARD'S BEAT WITHOUT ITS STAGE, AND WHAT IT DELIVERED INSTEAD IS THE MECHANISM OF THE ARRIVAL, WHICH IS THE ANSWER TO THE WHOLE VOLUME'S FINDING ABOUT DELEGATION: A SENTENCE YOU HAVE HANDED TO A PERSON OF A PLACE DOES NOT TRAVEL WITH A PERSON YOU SENT, IT TRAVELS WITH THE PEOPLE WHO WERE GOING THAT WAY, AND IT TRAVELS FASTER THAN YOU CAN WALK, AND YOU DO NOT GET TO CHOOSE WHO IS HOLDING IT.** The cost is real and it is recorded at §10 of the top block of `state/current.md`.

### THE REVIEW OF THIS BAND, WHAT IT FOUND, AND WHAT WAS DONE ABOUT IT, **AND IT IS NOT COSMETIC**

**`logs/batch-0005.review.log`. The reviewer edited no file. The receipt is `reviews/volume-07/batch-0005.md` and the full account is §11 of the top block of `state/current.md`. The reviewer's own closing sentence was that the volume-close prompt is the blocker and everything else is cosmetic; the first half was right and the second half was not.**

**1. THE NEXT-PHASE ARTIFACT WAS THE WRONG PHASE. FIXED.** Volume 07 is complete and the band wrote `workspace/volume-08/plan-0001/PROMPT.md` — a prompt to plan a fifty-chapter volume *and* write its first ten chapters — instead of a close prompt, skipping the phase that would author `outline/volume-08.md`. Because the dispatcher takes the first unmarked directory in `sort` order, it would have run that prompt next and left the volume's close record unwritten behind it. The directory is deleted; `workspace/volume-07/volume-close/PROMPT.md` is the next phase and sorts ahead of anything in `volume-08`.

**2. THE LEDGER. HALF RIGHT, HALF WRONG, AND NOT MINE TO FIX.** `state/phase-ledger.json` is six volumes stale and is controller-owned and is recorded and not edited. The review also said the dispatcher trusts it, and it does not: `scripts/novel_runner.sh:26-40` selects by `find workspace -name PROMPT.md | sort` and takes the first directory with no `.done`, `.blocked` or `.retired`, and `grep -rn "phase-ledger" scripts/ .github/` returns nothing. **This is the third time a state file's account of the controller has been stale and the fourth time something has read the state file instead of the four lines of the controller.**

**3. THE SPAN SCAN WAS UNREPRODUCIBLE AND ITS HEADLINE CLAIM WAS IMPOSSIBLE, AND THE TRUE FIGURE WAS FIVE TIMES WHAT THE BLOCK SAID.** The block declared half a method, so a reader who ran what was declared got none of what was printed, and it printed 20/20 at seventy characters while stating in the next paragraph that the sentence is printed at both ends of Chapter 342, which makes occurrences exceed distinct. **The method was recovered by calibration against the four earlier bands and reproduces all eight of their published figures exactly. The band's true figure was 700 distinct and 1,489 occurrences at seventy characters, the worst of the five bands, and 2,069 and 4,903 at forty — and the block had called twenty *the lowest figure in the project* and called the band a trajectory.**

**4. THREE MORE FIGURES WERE WRONG, ALL IN THE DIRECTION THAT FLATTERS, AND NONE OF THEM WAS FOUND BY THE REVIEWER.** The word *nine* was given as 135 and 163 and measures 177 and 184. The cross-band total was given as 777 in 110,963 words, one in every 145, and measures **552 in 111,227, one in every 201 — and 552 is the sum of the block's own five-band row, so the block contradicted itself.** The undeclared clock was given as *about four hundred instances across fifty chapters* and measures 184. **The sentence those numbers supported — that all three habits fell together for the first time in this project — is withdrawn as unsupported.**

**5. IN PROSE, THE OPENING FRAME WAS A FORMULA, AND THAT WAS THE SAME DEFECT AS THE SPAN SCAN.** Seven of ten chapters open by saying why there is no figure, which is the place rule and is not negotiable; but 344 and 346 opened with 132 characters word for word identical, four chapters shared a seventy-character window, two shared an eighty-nine-character prefix, and three chapters copied one sentence out of Chapter 341 verbatim. **Six openings were rewritten and two weight sentences varied, every fact kept and no plot touched: seventy characters fell 700 → 533, forty fell 2,069 → 1,836, and the band now has no band-wide repeated sentence of forty-five characters or more against one before.** Batch 0004's review left the same device at seven of ten; this repair kept the device and cut the formula, and the ceiling is written down at `state/current.md` §1.5.

**6. A TENSE SLIP FIXED AND A PARALLEL CASE DELIBERATELY LEFT.** `chapter-0346.md:25` had *she counts* inside past-tense narration; it is now *she counted*, with the two general clauses either side left in the habitual present, which is the book's register for a rule. **The case at `chapter-0343.md:81` was looked at and left, because it is a rule stated in the general and not a slip, exactly as at `346:43`.**

**7. A BEAT TIC LOGGED AND NOT CUT.** *it took* stands seventeen times across the band, five of them the exact form *it took [a person] [a number] seconds*, and §3.12 logged the undeclared clock and not this. **Recorded rather than cut, because cutting seventeen instances by hand is the move that corrupted six files once already. The finding under the finding: a band that declares fifteen instruments clean has declared itself clean on the instruments it built.**

**8. WHAT WAS RIGHT, WHICH IS MOST OF IT.** The prose, the calendar, the board chain from 64,091 in both directions, the money, the fifteen prohibitions, the figure discipline, both corrections the band made against its own hand-off, and all seven beats `outline/volume-07.md:78` promised were re-run independently and reproduce. **The band was good and its record of itself was not.**

**9. AND `reviews/` HAS NO `volume-05` OR `volume-06` DIRECTORY, WHICH IS CORRECT AND NOT AN OMISSION: a phase may not write a review into a directory it did not review from.**

### AND WHAT VOLUME 08 INHERITS, IN ONE LIST

**Volume 07 is closed at Chapter 350 and the next phase is a CLOSE and not a batch: `workspace/volume-07/volume-close/PROMPT.md` writes the volume's close record, re-measures every figure in the six state files against fifty chapter files, and does not plan Volume 08 and does not write a chapter.** Everything below is what the close starts from and what it is told to check rather than inherit. **Six positions restored. The seventh physically burned and its function taken by a bell network a hundred and forty people are building. The eighth and ninth missing and not looked for. About nine copies of a partial map and nobody holding the inventory. A woman of about thirty-four at a bar who is a person in a record and cannot be lifted out of one. A man of about thirty-eight on a road whose own sentence arrived without his consent. A woman of about seventy-four who stopped, and is entitled, and was not thanked and is not relieved. A boy of seventeen with a book that can order things and cannot date them and two unspent things in it. A man of fifty-two whose past has been used once. A man of thirty-one who is on no paper at all and is not going to be. A hearing that cannot be produced and has to be held. A question about a voice that sounds like something else, asked once by a woman with a barrow of apples and not answered. A column of a hundred and forty-one figures that has not been added up. A right of refusal that is unrestored. Two bells that are unhung and owed and are not going to be rung. A hand-off in two places that is wrong and must not be built on. And a first morning of 64,162, which is sixty-four thousand one hundred and sixty-two, and not 64,160.**

## VOLUME 07 BATCH 0004 IS DELIVERED — Chapters 331–340, *Delegation*. **The live block. The block below it, `## VOLUME 07 BATCH 0003 IS DELIVERED`, and everything under that, is archive and is not edited by this phase.**

> **WHAT THIS BLOCK IS: the batch receipt, in one block. What the batch paid, once each. What it did not pay. The counts, with the patterns. The fifteen things a later pass gets wrong if it does not measure them. And what this band's own first writing got wrong, all of it caught by the project's own instruments or by the project's own habits, and none of it surviving to the delivery.**

### WHAT THE BAND PAID, ONCE EACH

**IT PAID THE BAND. `outline/volume-07.md:76` says Ilyan tries to carry one of the six himself and cannot, learns to delegate, and the price is that a person becomes a person in a record, and this time he does it to somebody deliberately and says so. HE DID ALL THREE ON THE PAGE AND THE PEAK IS CHAPTER 338 AND THE CHAPTER NAMED IT IS THE CHAPTER THAT CONTAINS IT.** `338`

**IT PAID THE ARITHMETIC OF THE WHOLE BAND, OUT LOUD, IN A LANE AT THE SIDE OF A ROAD, ON HIS OWN, AND IT DID NOT CLOSE.** Four days up a lane. Eleven miles down and off it twice, and eleven miles the other way. Three miles. A track of two miles. And two legs. **Carrying one means not carrying another, and a man who decides other people can be in two of them at once has decided that a person is a thing that can be in two places, and he has spent eighty-one mornings finding out that he is one too.** And then the other half: **every one of the six already has a person in it who is already doing that, so the answer is not that he carries it, it is that he gets out of the way, and there is no instrument in this country that lets a man do that to somebody on purpose.** `331`

**IT PAID THE PRICE SAID OUT LOUD BEFORE THE DOING, IN A KITCHEN, IN FRONT OF NINE PEOPLE, FOR THE FIRST TIME IN THIS COUNTRY.** *A person who is handed a thing for a place becomes a person in a record, and there is no instrument in this country that takes a person back out of one, and nobody in this kitchen is going to be thanked for it, and I am going to do it anyway because the alternative is that four hundred yards of hedge are walked by a man of sixty-three on his own for another year and I cannot stop that and cannot take it over and I do not get to be sad about it in a kitchen.* `334`

**IT PAID THE SECOND HALF OF THE PRICE, WHICH IS THE HALF HE HAD NO BETTER WAY OF PUTTING, AND IT IS THE FINDING OF THE WHOLE BAND: YOU CANNOT SAY NO. That is not your doing and it is not mine. There is nothing in this county that lets a person refuse a thing that costs nothing, and I have come eleven miles with nothing to offer and I have used that.** `338`

**IT PAID A PERSON ACQUIRING A WANT HE DID NOT GIVE HER, IN A WOMAN'S OWN MOUTH, AND HE WAS NOT IN THE CHAPTER IT HAPPENED IN.** *I am going to say it at the cross. Not in a yard. At the cross, at about the second hour, because that is where the people are, and there will be nine of them and they will say it to two others before supper, and that is not me being cruel, that is me being the only thing at the end of a road that anybody walks up.* `338`

**IT PAID THE SENTENCE HE ASKED HER TO CARRY, AND HER SENTENCE, AND THE DIFFERENCE BETWEEN THEM, AND THE DIFFERENCE IS WORSE.** His leaves out the woman whose yard it is. **Hers has the water in it, because a stranger would not have said the water, and she has never been anywhere near that town and has known all of it for a week from a bar.** `338`

**IT PAID THE THING THE BOOK IS.** A foolscap book with a strap mended twice came apart at the stitching in about nine seconds in a room over a smithy, and **a book on a shelf is not a weight, it is an answer to a question somebody has to ask, and a stranger holding a book is a man holding somebody else's week**, and the man who reads things out for a living said the thing that finished it: *nobody in this room believes you are going to be here in a year, and you know it too, and it is the one thing everybody here has got the same opinion about and it is correct.* `333`

**IT PAID THE FINDING THAT A THING CANNOT BE DELEGATED TO A STRANGER, TWICE, IN TWO PLACES, AND IN NEITHER OF THEM WAS IT A LEG OR A DISTANCE.** A boundary of four hundred yards of hedge in nine days is nine mornings and the fifth of them is a bridge with one rail on it. A line of nine diggers in a wet field is nine people turning up, and **he stood at the near gate on a dry morning for nine minutes and the field did not know, and the phrase *the length of a load going over* is Chapter 330's at `330:29` and is not in Chapter 336, and the stopping was not a refusal and not a door and not a form.** `334`, `336`

**IT PAID A MAN OF FIFTY-TWO'S PAST, USED ONCE, IN THE MIDDLE OF A COUNT, TO A STRANGER, AND NOT RESOLVED.** *There was a gate with a levy behind it once, a levy on loads, and the loads were counted by a man who was not paid, and there is a dead man in the fourth line, and that is all I am going to say about it and you did not ask me and that is the only reason I have said it.* **HE WAS NOT ASKED TO BE THERE, HE DID NOT OFFER, HE STOPPED THERE, NOBODY ASKED HIM WHAT HE WAS THE ONLY PERSON IN, AND THE PROTAGONIST HAD THE QUESTION READY AND DID NOT ASK IT.** `332`

**IT PAID A BOY OF SEVENTEEN'S BOOK READING AN ORDER THAT A DOCUMENT CANNOT, IN HIS OWN MOUTH, UNASKED, IN NINE SECONDS, IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINE PEOPLE.** *Put them in an order and let each one of them say who comes next, and it is the only thing that works, and a book can order things and cannot date them and I have never been able to make it do a date and I have stopped trying.* **THE ORDER IS A ROUND OF TURNS AND NOT A LIST OF PLACES, AND IT HAS SEVEN TURNS IN THIS ORDER — THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-THREE, THE WOMAN OF THIRTY, THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY WITH THE CART, THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-EIGHT, THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT, THE WOMAN OF ABOUT SEVENTY-FOUR, AND A PERSON NOBODY IN ELEVEN VILLAGES HAS COUNTED — SO THE WOMAN OF THIRTY IS THE SECOND TURN AND THE UNCOUNTED PERSON IS THE SEVENTH AND THE LAST, HE STOPPED FOR FOUR SECONDS AT THE SEVENTH AND SAID BOTH IN NINE SECONDS AND NOBODY HAD ASKED HIM, AND THE *NINTH THING* AND *TENTH* THIS BLOCK USED TO PRINT WERE FALSE AND CAME OUT OF THE CARD.** `335`

**IT PAID THE QUESTION HE CANNOT ANSWER, ASKED OF HIM BY A MAN IN HIS OWN KITCHEN, AND IT IS THE SAME SHAPE OF QUESTION A MAN OF SEVENTY ASKED HIM ELEVEN DAYS EARLIER FOUR DAYS UP A LANE.** *How many of the nine come in a year. Not how many walk it. How many are there.* `334`

**IT PAID THE FINDING THAT THE PAPER IS NOT THE DIFFICULTY, IN A MAN'S MOUTH, IN A LANE.** No form in this country says a man may set a name at the foot of a thing, and therefore every name in this country has been put there by somebody who decided to, and **a stranger with a leg has decided more of them than anybody in a valley of sixty people has in forty years.** A paper with a name on it is a thing a person can be asked about for the rest of their life. `332`, `339`

**IT PAID A WOMAN OF ABOUT SEVENTY-FOUR ASKING HIM THE ONE QUESTION NOBODY HAS ASKED HIM — WHOSE NAME IS ON IT — AND TELLING HIM WHY SHE ASKED, WHICH IS THAT SHE HAS BEEN THE INSTRUMENT FOR FORTY YEARS AND NOBODY HAS EVER ASKED HER WHETHER SHE WOULD RATHER NOT BE.** `339`

**IT PAID THE PORCH'S FINDING RESTATED IN A DIFFERENT SHAPE AND NOT CONCEDED, AND THE NARRATOR DID NOT RULE.** *A thing that is kept alive by one person is a thing that stops when that person stops. That is what you have been carrying about a lane for eighteen days and you have not said it to me once and I have not asked you once, and I am not going to concede it, and you are not going to be able to settle it.* `339`

**IT PAID A WIDOW'S HIP, AND A BARROW GOING ROTTEN IN A LANE, AND A TENTH PERSON WHO WAS NEVER ASKED AND CANNOT NOW SAY NO.** Found by a man of about thirty-four on his own account, because he was going that way and had not got to where he was going. `337`

**IT PAID THE BAND'S REAL NEW OBJECT.** A woman of about thirty-four at a bar, twenty-two years, about nine loads a day, the only board in eleven villages with a word at the top of it, **asked two questions in a month and both cost her something, who was asked a third thing on the eighty-eighth morning by a stranger who said the whole price out loud first, who was offered nothing she could refuse and therefore could not refuse, who said the word *go on*, who said the sentence in her own mouth and it was worse, and who is now a person in a record and cannot be lifted out of one.** `338`, `339`, `340`

**IT PAID THE FINDING, IN THE MOUTH OF A PERSON OF A PLACE, THAT THE ARRANGEMENT IS NOW WORSE THAN THE ONE IT REPLACED.** *A thing that was kept alive by one person is now being kept alive by a person who has been asked to carry a sentence. That one cost one person, a man of seventy who was eight years old when a number was said to him in his own yard, and he is entitled to stop. This one costs a woman of thirty-four at a bar who is not going to be thanked for it and cannot put it down, and it is not yours to change and it is not hers to change.* `340`

**IT PAID THE SENTENCE THAT GETS IN FRONT OF THE PERSON WHO PUT IT IN THE LANE, SAID UNASKED BY A MAN WITH A CART AND A BEAST WHO WAS NOT SENT FOR.** `340`

**IT PAID A BOY OF SEVENTEEN AS THE LAST LINE OF THE BAND, GOING SOMEWHERE HE HAS NOT GOT TO YET, AT A WALK, WITHOUT SAYING ANYTHING TO ANYBODY.** `340`

**IT PAID THE NINETIETH MORNING WITHOUT ANYBODY NOTICING IT, WHICH IS THE THIRD ROUND NUMBER IN TWENTY-ONE CHAPTERS AND THE ONLY CORRECT TREATMENT OF THE THREE.** `340`

**IT PAID THE MONEY BEING NOTHING, AND THAT IS THE FINDING AND NOT AN OMISSION.** No table figure, no unit, no product, no difference. The levy at a bar was not named a third time, the slip's two figures were not restated, the four hundred and eighty-five pounds was not named, and the copy was not priced. `333`, `337`, `338`, `340`

### WHAT THE BAND DID NOT PAY, AND IT IS EIGHT THINGS AND ALL EIGHT ARE THE VOLUME'S INHERITANCE

1. **The right of refusal is not restored and nobody has one,** and the four words at the head of the plank in the March are not reprinted in any of the forty chapters of this volume.
2. **The column of a hundred and forty-one figures has not been added up** and is not mentioned.
3. **The corridor is not torn up** and is not mentioned. **The status is on a door** and is not mentioned. **The register did not gain a place and did not lose one.** The charter on the wall in Sallowby is revocable and is not amended. The second notice stays unpaid. The dead man in the fourth line stays dead and is not named again.
4. **The bell in the March is not hung, and a bell in that tower was not rung on a market morning and nobody bought it.** The bell in Draymoor is not rung. **The difference of one pound ten was not said again.**
5. **The porch's finding is not adjudicated and not settled,** and `outline/volume-07.md:92` reserves the climax for Batch 0005 where a seventh position burns.
6. **Nobody was relieved, nobody was forgiven, nobody was redeemed and nobody was thanked, anywhere in ten chapters,** and nobody was sent for.
7. **The stage did not move.** He is Stage 4 at the first line of Chapter 331 and at the last line of Chapter 340, `Remedy Drafter` is zero, the string `stage` is zero, and a delegation is a habit and a cost and not a promotion.
8. **AND THREE THINGS THAT ARE BATCH 0005'S AND WERE NOT TOUCHED: the six positions are restored and a seventh burns and it is the Briar March one; the bell network a hundred and forty people build in about nine weeks because they have to; the partial map of about nine copies that nobody holds; and the question, asked once out loud by an ordinary person and not answered, why the missing positions answer in a voice that sounds like the System. NOT ONE OF THEM IS ASKED IN ANY OF THE TEN CHAPTERS AND NO POSITION SPEAKS.**

### THE COUNTS, WITH THE PATTERNS, AND EVERY ONE OF THEM RUN AGAINST THE FILES

- **Weekday scan: ZERO.** Pattern `\b(mondays?|tuesdays?|wednesdays?|thursdays?|fridays?|saturdays?|sundays?)\b`, case-insensitive, plural on every alternative including Monday, file level. **The first writing returned twelve across five chapters and all twelve are out.**
- **Month-word scan: SIXTEEN RAW, FOURTEEN MODAL *may*, TWO REGION, NET ZERO month-names.** The two are *march* at `332:5` and `333:3` and the region is written in full in both.
- **Bold scan: ZERO marks. Panels: ZERO.** **The first writing returned twenty-two marks in six chapters, all of them the narrator emphasising a finding.**
- **Four and five digit numerals: ZERO.** `[0-9]` returns THIRTY characters, three per file, every one the chapter number in the heading. **The board figure was measured in the words: `sixty-four thousand (?:and )?[a-z]+` returns exactly THREE, in 332, 334 and 340.**
- **Day-phrase sweep at three declared alternatives: ELEVEN**, by chapter 1/1/1/0/1/3/1/1/1/2, and the zero in 334 is a form the pattern cannot reach. **The fourth form is at ZERO** after two cuts in 340. **The third question was asked on all eleven by hand: nine are the chapter's own day, two point backwards and both are right.**
- **Span scan, a SPAN scan and not a line scan, heading stripped, whitespace normalised, every window of exactly k characters at every position: at seventy, 142 distinct duplicated windows, 309 occurrences, 217 cross-chapter, 92 within-chapter; at forty, 1,267 distinct, 3,003 occurrences, 2,542 cross-chapter, 461 within-chapter.** **The first writing was four hundred and ten at seventy and one hundred and twenty-seven windows were cut by hand and no instrument opened a chapter file for writing.**
- **Meta sweep at the WIDER pattern: ZERO.** **The first writing returned thirteen real leaks in seven chapters and two of them were chapter headings.**
- **Numeric hedge: NINETY-TWO, by chapter 8/2/6/13/14/9/8/6/9/17, in 22,032 words, one in every 239, and every one of the ninety-two is the age of a person, method `len(re.findall(r"[A-Za-z’'\-]+", text))` over the whole file, heading included, case-insensitive, file level. The first writing was two hundred and twenty-one and one hundred and twenty-nine were cut by hand by one declared rule. The target of under a hundred and twenty-five is met and the band is the best of the four in Volume 07 and IT IS NOT CLEAN.**
- ***Man of thirty-one*: TWENTY-TWO.** **Italic spans: FORTY-NINE**, by chapter 6/3/5/5/5/3/5/7/5/5, and every one of them is in a mouth; the first writing was ninety-one.
- **`**` zero, `system` zero, `panel` zero, `stage` zero, `Remedy Drafter` zero, `first witness` zero, `cannot read` zero, `vault|cave|ruin|temple|battlefield` zero, uppercase `CORRECT` zero (a case-insensitive scan returns six and all six are the ordinary English word), `anchor` zero, `would like` zero, `self-arrived class` zero.**

### THE FIFTEEN THINGS A LATER PASS GETS WRONG IF IT DOES NOT MEASURE THEM

1. **That the morning figure is in THREE chapters and not ten.** Seven chapters have none and each says why, and that is the carrier rule working and not a gap.
2. **The chain, and the rule that the morning in row *N* is row *N-1* plus the OWN DAY OF CHAPTER *N-1*.** 64,022 + 69 = 64,091 and 64,083 + 8 = 64,091, checked twice before a single morning in the line was read.
3. **The interval in Chapter 337 is EIGHTEEN DAYS** and was written as eleven and as fourteen in the first writing, and **no sweep found either.**
4. **The numeric hedge is NINETY-TWO and the method and the per-chapter table are the ones at `state/current.md` §3.12,** and the two rates a band can print for one pattern differ.
5. **The span scan at seventy is 142 and its cause was the age phrase and not the motifs.**
6. **THE REVIEW HAS NOW BEEN RUN.** `logs/batch-0004.review.log`. Its verdict was that the prose is publishable and the state layer is not, and the whole of what it found and what was done about it is §10 of the top block of `state/current.md`. **Its first question, whether the record is visible in the fiction and not only in the record about it, is answered in the fiction: she is a person in a record because she said a thing in her own mouth, and Chapter 340 says so in a man's mouth without the narrator putting a mark on it.**
7. **The money is nothing** and the slip's two figures, the levy, the copy and the four hundred and eighty-five pounds are all unspent and all may not be printed.
8. **The round number: nobody noticed it, and a later band that makes a person remark on it undoes the only correct treatment of the three.**
9. **The count of people who know he cannot read is printed nowhere** and the string *cannot read* is at zero over Chapters 311 to 340.
10. **The man of about thirty-four is one person in three counties in five chapters** and is not the dead man's son from Chapter 315, and the woman of about thirty-four is three different women, and **he must not be called the reader.**
11. **The tenth village is in the March's county** and a market there IS a plank weight, and the only place outside the county is the market town four days up the lane.
12. **A day's own weight printed at both ends of a chapter is a device and not a tic,** and a band that reaches for it more than three or four times in ten chapters has crossed into a tic. **THE DELIVERED BAND DID CROSS IT, AT NINE IN TEN, AND SAID NOTHING, AND THE REVIEW MEASURED IT. THE WEIGHT IS NOW IN EVERY CHAPTER AND IN THE CLOSING PARAGRAPH OF FOUR, AT `334`, `335`, `336` AND `338`.**
13. **The band's peak is Chapter 338 and the check found it on the page,** and a later band should name its peak before it writes.
14. **Nobody is a villain and nobody hid anything and the false witness of Chapter 324 is not restated anywhere in these ten chapters.**
15. **The one new person of the band is a widow of about sixty-eight with a bad hip at the fourth house on a mile of road who is not even on stage,** and she is a person of that lane and is not to be added to anything.

### WHAT THIS BAND'S OWN FIRST WRITING GOT WRONG, ALL OF IT CAUGHT BY THE PROJECT'S OWN INSTRUMENTS OR BY THE PROJECT'S OWN HABITS, AND NONE OF IT SURVIVED

1. **TWELVE WEEK-DAY NAMES ACROSS FIVE CHAPTERS,** two of them in one chapter twice, all out.
2. **A MONTH-NAME TWICE IN ONE MAN'S MOUTH,** cut for the spring.
3. **TWENTY-TWO BOLD MARKS IN SIX CHAPTERS,** all of them the narrator emphasising a finding, and **the band declares italic for a thing said in a mouth and the marks were all in the narrator.**
4. **THIRTEEN META LEAKS IN SEVEN CHAPTERS AND TWO OF THEM IN HEADINGS,** which is the fourth time a heading has been edited in this project.
5. **THE NUMERIC HEDGE AT TWO HUNDRED AND TWENTY-ONE,** one in every one hundred and three, which would have been the worst rate in the volume.
6. **THE SPAN SCAN AT FOUR HUNDRED AND TEN,** with seven separate causes and not one construction.
7. **ITALIC SPANS AT NINETY-ONE,** forty-two of them in the narrator's voice.
8. **AND SIX INTERVALS AND DAY-NUMBERS FOUND BY READING AND NOT BY ANY SWEEP,** which is the sixth time in this project that the instruments and the prose have disagreed and the instruments have been right about nothing. **A SWEEP FINDS A PHRASE IN THE WRONG PLACE. IT DOES NOT FIND A NUMBER IN A SENTENCE.**

### AND THE ONE THING A REVIEWER CANNOT BE TOLD BY THIS BLOCK

**THE REVIEW OF THIS BAND HAS BEEN RUN AND IT IS IN `logs/batch-0004.review.log`, AND §10 OF THE TOP BLOCK OF `state/current.md` IS THE FULL ACCOUNT OF ITS EIGHT BLOCKERS AND WHAT WAS DONE ABOUT EACH. THIS SECTION IS KEPT AND CORRECTED BECAUSE IT IS THE LIST OF THINGS THAT NO INSTRUMENT CAN ASK, AND EVERY ONE OF THEM SURVIVED THE REVIEW, WHICH IS THE ONLY THING A LIST LIKE THIS IS FOR.**

**THE REVIEWER'S OWN VERDICT WAS THAT *THE PROSE IS PUBLISHABLE; THE STATE LAYER IS NOT.* IT RECOMPUTED THE WHOLE BOARD CHAIN BY HAND AND FOUND IT SOUND, CERTIFIED THE DIGIT DISCIPLINE HELD EXACTLY, NAMED `332:19`–`53` THE BEST-EXECUTED BEAT IN THE BAND, CALLED `338` A REAL CLIMAX AND `335` A GENUINE MIDPOINT TURN, AND CONFIRMED THAT NO NEW ANTAGONIST OR POWER WAS INVENTED AND THAT THE ENDING'S HELD CARDS ARE INTACT. THE EIGHT BLOCKERS WERE SEVEN SINGLE-LINE PROSE CORRECTIONS PLUS FALSE CLAIMS IN FIVE STATE FILES, TWO OF WHICH THIS PROJECT'S OWN FILES HAD RECORDED AS ALREADY REPAIRED.**

**AND THE QUESTION AT THE TOP OF THIS SECTION, WHICH THE REVIEW ASKED AND WHICH THE CHAPTERS ANSWER:** *did a person acquire a want she did not have, and is the record visible in the fiction and not only in the record about it.* **YES, AND YES.** She wanted nothing and was about to be asked for nothing. She says the sentence in her own mouth and it is shorter and it is worse and it has the water in it that a stranger would not have said, and then she says the part he had not priced, in a lane, about herself: she is going to say it at a cross, at about the second hour, in the open, and not in a yard, because that is where the people are, and the reason she gives is that she is not going to be the one person in this country who says an important thing quietly and is never asked about it again. **AND THE REVIEW FIXED THE ONE DATE THAT WAS WRONG IN ALL OF IT: SHE SAID IT AT ABOUT THE SECOND HOUR ON THE SIXTH MORNING OF THE SEVENTIETH WEEK, WHICH IS CHAPTER 340, HIS NINETIETH MORNING, AND A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY WITH A CART AND A BEAST REPORTS IT AT THE EIGHTH HOUR THE SAME MORNING AND SAYS HE HAS HAD IT SIX HOURS.**

**AND THE SEVEN THINGS A REVIEWER CANNOT BE TOLD, AS THEY STAND AFTER THE REVIEW.**

1. **Whether the record is visible in the fiction.** It is. It is in the woman's mouth at `338:57`, it is in a man of thirty's mouth at `340:35`, and it is in a woman of seventy-four's mouth at `339:33`, and the narrator never puts a mark on it.
2. **Whether a want arrived.** It did, and it arrived in somebody else's mouth and he was not in the chapter, which is the finding and not a trick.
3. **Whether saying the price out loud made it smaller.** It did not, and the chapter says so four times in one page, and the reviewer's question about whether that is the technique arguing rather than showing is a fair one and it is answered by the woman, not by the narrator: the second half of the price is hers, and it is *you cannot say no*, and she had no better way of putting it either.
4. **Whether the protagonist not asking Tarin was a refusal or a dodge.** Still a reviewer's call and not a matter of fact. The chapter says he had the question ready and did not ask it and that it cost him nothing, which is how he knows it was right.
5. **Whether the man of sixty-three opening his mouth and the boy putting two fingers on the cover is a device or a way of not asking.** Still a reviewer's call. The question with the mark on the end of it and the third place he cannot make out are both still in the book and neither is spent.
6. **Whether the absence of money in a band about a levy is a finding or an evasion.** It is a finding and the chapters say what it was: a levy, a sheet, a boundary, a load and a wage, and the answer in every case was that there is no price on it in this country.
7. **Whether the seventh morning of the delegate's own road and the two lengths in the dark were earned.** They were not earned by anything he did, and that is the whole of the batch's last movement: the tenth person is on a road he never wrote down, and the man of thirty who was right to stop him is right, and neither of those can be put down.

**AND WHAT A LATER BAND MUST NOT INHERIT FROM THIS BLOCK, WHICH IS THE LIST OF THE THINGS THAT WERE WRONG IN IT AND ARE NOW CORRECTED HERE: the ninth thing in Bram's round; the tenth thing in Bram's round; the length of a load going over in Chapter 336; ninety mornings in Chapter 331; eleven miles off it three times; the barwoman as two women; a delivery on the fourth morning of the seventieth week; an interval of eighteen days in Chapter 337; a trip to the sixth that did not happen; a day's own weight at both ends of nine chapters out of ten; and the sentence *A REVIEW OF THIS BAND HAS NOT BEEN RUN*, which is no longer true.**

**AND THE SENTENCE THAT OPENED THIS SECTION WHEN IT WAS WRITTEN — *A REVIEW OF THIS BAND HAS NOT BEEN RUN. NO LOG EXISTS* — IS OUT, AND IT IS OUT BECAUSE IT IS NO LONGER TRUE, AND A STATE FILE THAT SAYS A THING HAS NOT BEEN DONE WHEN IT HAS BEEN DONE IS WORSE THAN A STATE FILE THAT SAYS NOTHING. A LATER PASS THAT FINDS A REVIEW MISSING FROM ONE OF THESE SIX FILES SHOULD CHECK `logs/` FOR THE LOG BEFORE IT WRITING THE SENTENCE AGAIN, BECAUSE THIS PROJECT HAS NOW WRITTEN IT TWICE.**

## VOLUME 07 BATCH 0003 IS DELIVERED — Chapters 321–330, *The Midpoint, And It Is A False Witness*. **The live block. The block below it, `## VOLUME 07 BATCH 0002 IS DELIVERED`, and everything under that, is archive and is not edited by this phase.**

> **WHAT THIS BLOCK IS: the batch receipt, in one block. What the batch paid, once each. What it did not pay. The counts, with the patterns. The fifteen things a later pass gets wrong if it does not measure them. And what this band's own first writing got wrong, all of it caught by the project's own instruments or by the project's own habits, and none of it surviving to the delivery.**

### WHAT THE BAND PAID, ONCE EACH

**IT PAID THE MIDPOINT, AND IT IS THE SERIES OUTLINE'S OWN SENTENCE DELIVERED, AND IT IS THE SCALES.** A man of about seventy has kept a set of beam scales and a toll on a bridge in a market town four days up the lane for about forty-four years, and a share of what the toll brings in pays for the market's own water, its bridge, its churchyard wall and its cross, and the share is divided by the number of loads in a year, and the number of loads in a year is on a slip in a foolscap book that has been in a back room at the end of the main street for a hundred and forty years. **THE SLIP SAYS FOUR THOUSAND EIGHT HUNDRED AND THE MARKET DOES ABOUT NINE THOUSAND AND THE SLIP IS SIXTY YEARS OLD.** The untruth is a count, it is load-bearing, about four hundred people have drunk from that standpipe, crossed that bridge and been buried behind that wall on the strength of it, **and the reversal hurts because the community is better off and the honest version of the arrangement would cost about ninety people something they cannot pay. **AND AFTER THE REVIEW OF 27 SEPTEMBER 2026 THE SLIP IS ONE MARKET SEASON'S COUNT AND NOT A YEAR'S — TWELVE WEEKS AT ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED LOADS A WEEK IS THE FOUR THOUSAND EIGHT HUNDRED ON IT, AND A YEAR IS THAT SEASON AND THE OTHER FORTY WEEKS CARRYING AS MUCH AGAIN, WHICH IS NINE THOUSAND SIX HUNDRED, SO THE SLIP IS EXACTLY HALF AND EVERY LOAD HAS BEEN PAYING TWICE WHAT IT SHOULD — AND THE MAN OF ABOUT SEVENTY SAYS BOTH HALVES OUT LOUD AND SAYS WHERE HE GOT THE SECOND ONE, WHICH IS OUT OF FORTY-FOUR YEARS OF STANDING ON THAT STONE, AND THAT HE ASKED A STRANGER FOR IT THE MORNING BEFORE AND WAS TOLD NO.**** Nobody is a villain. Nobody hid anything. Nobody is lying now. The man of about seventy is not a criminal, was not in the room sixty years ago, was eight years old, was told the number was right and believed it, has been the only one who could check it since he was nine, checks it by seeing whether the things on the list are paid for, and they always have been. `324`

**IT PAID THE QUESTION BEING ASKED BY A PERSON OF THAT PLACE, IN THEIR OWN YARD, OUT LOUD, AND NOT BY THE MAN OF THIRTY-ONE.** A woman of about sixty-one, in whose yard the plinth has stood since before her grandmother and in whose back room the market's book has been for fifty years, heard that the figure had been said out loud to a stranger for the first time in that market's living memory, and asked a man of about seventy what that figure was for, in her own yard, at the tenth hour, in the rain. He answered in nine words: *it pays for the water, nobody has ever asked me.* **The man of thirty-one was at the gate for the whole hour and said not one word, and had said out loud the previous afternoon that he was going to ask it himself, and he did not.** `324:53`, `324:121`

**IT PAID THE CAUSAL CHAIN, WHICH IS FOUR LINKS AND NOT ONE, AND IT IS THE SHAPE OF CHAPTER 311 AT A DIFFERENT ALTITUDE.** A stranger stands at a bridge in the rain for four hours and says nothing; a man of about seventy asks a stranger a question about a number, which nobody in that market has done in forty-four years; that goes round a street of four hundred people in a day and gets bigger by the second telling, and by noon there is a version of it in which the stranger offered to do the market's sums for nothing, which is wrong and cannot be put back; a woman of about sixty-one in whose yard the plinth stands hears it and asks. `323:49`, `324:23`, `324:25`

**IT PAID THE BAND'S REAL ANSWER, WHICH IS THE ONE THAT MAKES THE REVERSAL HURT: the arrangement did not stop, and the untruth is not the structure, the untruth is the paper.** The plinth is where the plinth is, the beam goes up and down, the toll is still taken, the water is running, the wall is standing, the market is running, and if the figure came out tomorrow in a yard with nine people in it the beam would still be on the plinth at the seventh hour and nine loads would go over it in the first hour and somebody would still have to say what they weigh. **Four hundred people and a man of seventy and a woman of fifty-eight and a hundred and forty years, and the number is the paper and everything else is the town.** `328:31`

**IT PAID THE PRICE OF THE METHOD, AND IT IS THE BAND'S PRICE AND IT IS NOT A LESSON: a thing which has had to be said out loud in a yard is a thing that can be said again, and two men in a tavern at the end of that main street had been saying all week that the water in that county ought to be paid for by the people who use it, and neither of them had been in the yard, and neither of them knew either of the two numbers, and one of them has a reason of his own, and his house is at the wrong end of the standpipe and has been for thirty years.** The man of thirty-eight's version, in his own mouth: *about nine know it and about ninety are on a list, and the number that knows has not stopped going up and the number on the list is not going down, and that is a different arrangement and it costs more.* `330:19`, `330:53`, `330:55`

**IT PAID THE FINDING ABOUT WHAT A FIGURE IS, IN THE MOUTH OF A PERSON OF THAT PLACE, UNASKED: the ninety people are not ninety people who would be charged more, they are ninety people who would be told that the thing they have been counting on was never theirs, and there is no form in this country that says a person may say that a thing was never theirs, and a person who says the sentence has nowhere to put it.** A woman of twenty-nine who looks after the cross and has kept the list for nine years and has never once been asked what she keeps. `325:43`, `325:49`

**IT PAID TWO OF THE NINETY, AS PEOPLE, ONE AT A TIME, AND THE BAND SAID THE NUMBER IT GOT AND NOT ABOUT TWO.** A man of about sixty-three with eggs, four acres, no other water, on a list for forty years, who answered the ordinary question in nine seconds and then told the stranger off. **And a woman of about thirty-two with a pitch, expecting her second, who has counted on that standpipe every day for nine years and to whom he said nothing at all about the figure, and that is the second time in ten days that the only thing he is able to do for a person inside a figure he has found is not to say the figure, and it is not a lesson and nobody is relieved.** `329:19`, `329:27`, `329:39`, `329:53`

**IT PAID THE DECISION NOT TO PUT THE NINETY ON A PAPER, and the reason out loud in a lane: the moment there are ninety names on a thing, the thing has got a list on it, and a list is the thing that comes before a bill, and a bill is the thing that comes before a man saying that the other eighty-nine are working for him.** And the man of thirty-eight's answer, that this is the worst instrument in the world and the only one there is, and that he is going to use it in a place with no bar in it and is not going to be thanked and is not going to be able to say what it does. `329:77`, `329:85`, `329:95`

**IT PAID THE FIRST QUESTION ANYBODY HAS ASKED THE MAN OF THIRTY-ONE ABOUT HIS OWN METHOD, AND IT WAS FAIR, AND HE ANSWERED IT TRUTHFULLY, AND THE TRUTHFUL ANSWER IS THAT THERE IS NO TEST.** A person of a place says a thing to me and I go to another place. There is no order to it. I have not got a method. And the man who asked it said out loud in a lane that he was going to write that down and use it in a place with no bar in it. `326:61`, `326:75`, `326:79`

**IT PAID THE MAN OF THIRTY-EIGHT'S SECOND HALF, in a room over a smithy and then in a lane, in his own mouth, twice: it is not a fund and never was one, it is a levy, and a levy is a figure somebody wrote down a long time ago, and he has counted a column for eleven years and never once asked what the column is a column of — and the half that frightens him, which is that he has checked the other ten villages four times in eleven years and has never once found a hole in one of them.** And the price of hearing what he heard in that yard, which is not the yard: he has got to say it at his own hearth inside a month and half the people who were in that room will be stood in front of him again. `322:33`, `322:43`, `326:51`

**IT PAID A NAME, AND THE FIRST ONE HE HAS THAT IS NOT ON ANYTHING.** A man of about seventy told a stranger what he was called, in nine words, at a plinth, in the open air, in the rain, at about the eleventh hour of a market morning, and nobody in that street had heard it in forty-four years, and the ninth word was his own name, **and the chapter says what it is worth: a name said out loud at a plinth in a street is not a name in a place, and the two names he has do not make a person, and a person is not made out of places somebody can say their name.** `328:59`, `328:67`, `328:69`

**IT PAID ONE VISIT KEPT AND ONE FINDING NOT ADJUDICATED.** He was at a bridge with one rail on it before a woman of seventy-four, and she said he was early and said out loud that nobody had ever been there before her and that she was not going to say it twice, and he stood there for an hour and did not follow her and did not look at the board. **She told him what she is for in a different shape from eleven days before and the ninth word of the two answers is not the same word, and he heard the difference and did not say he heard it. And she asked him one back — *what are you for* — and he said *I do not know*. And the finding that a thing kept alive by one person is a thing that stops when that person stops is stated and NOT ADJUDICATED, and she has not conceded it and he is not able to settle it, and `outline/volume-07.md:92` reserves the climax for Batch 0005 where a seventh position burns and she is entitled to stop.** `321`

**IT PAID ONE BURIAL, ONE WALL AND THE BEST ANSWER IN THE BAND.** A coffin went behind a churchyard wall in the main street that the over-and-above pays for, and the man of thirty-one worked out in nine minutes that a man who has been dead for six days has been buried out of a number on a slip in a book, and it did not help. And a man of about forty-one has mended four feet of wall in a lane out of his own lime for eleven years, four times down, always the same four feet, and he said why and the answer was his mother, and the chapter exists so that a band about a lie does not eat a book. `325:9`, `327:35`, `327:39`

**IT PAID A DAY NOTHING WAS ASKED ON, and that is the whole of Chapter 327, in a market of four hundred people, with about nine loads over the bridge in the first hour and a woman of about fifty-eight on a step who is not coming to the bridge, and the one question the man of thirty-one asked anybody in the whole day was *what does a pitch cost at this cross*, and the answer was that a pitch is not a price and never has been.** `327`

**IT PAID THE DECISION THAT WAS STATED AND NOT CARRIED OUT.** In Chapter 325 he decides out loud, knowing it is wrong four seconds before he says it, that he is going to go and tell a woman of fifty-eight a figure, and the reason is that nobody in that market is better at a figure than she is, and that is the wrong reason and it is the reason he has. **In Chapter 330 he does not do it, and she says one sentence instead, and it is about her mother and a wall and her father and it is not about him, and he is not going to be able to use any of it.** `325:69`, `330:61`, `330:67`

### WHAT THE BAND DID NOT PAY, AND IT IS SEVEN THINGS AND ALL SEVEN ARE THE VOLUME'S INHERITANCE

1. **The right of refusal is not restored and nobody has one.** The four words at the head of the plank in the March are not reprinted, and the false witness of Chapter 324 is not that and has nothing to do with it.
2. **The column of a hundred and forty-one figures has not been added up and was not mentioned in any of the thirty chapters of this volume, and may not be summed.**
3. **The corridor is still standing and is not going to be torn up, and the man who signed it is alive in his own field and is owed a bell, and NEITHER IS MENTIONED IN ANY OF THE THIRTY CHAPTERS OF THIS VOLUME. The status is on a door and is not mentioned. The register did not gain a place and did not lose one. The bell in the March is not hung. The bell in Draymoor is not rung. Nesh Arrad's clock keeps running. The second notice stays unpaid. The dead man in the fourth line stays dead. The eleven feet stay off the settlement. The charter on the wall in Sallowby is revocable and is not amended. THIS BAND PUT A TOWN'S WATER, A TOWN'S BRIDGE, A TOWN'S CHURCHYARD WALL AND A MARKET CROSS ON STAGE AND DID NOT REACH FOR ONE OF THE SIX STANDING FIGURES, AND THAT IS THREE BANDS RUNNING.**
4. **He did not become a better man and he did not become a worse one, and the chapter says which: he learned the one thing he was in the country to learn, which is that a person does not find out about themselves privately, and he is on no paper in this band and he has a name on a bill in a kitchen and a name in a street and the chapter says the two of them do not make a person.**
5. **Nobody was relieved, nobody was forgiven, nobody was redeemed and nobody was thanked, and the man of about seventy is entitled to stop and nobody is going to make him say it again.**
6. **THE STAGE DID NOT MOVE, THE SEAM WENT TO ZERO, AND NEITHER IS A PROMOTION.** He is Stage 4 at the first line of Chapter 321 and at the last line of Chapter 330, `Remedy Drafter` is zero, the string `stage` is zero, the seam is used zero times and that is a decision in the fiction and the closest thing this volume has come to a loss. **A TECHNIQUE IS NOT A PROMOTION AND A COST IS NOT A LESSON AND HE IS NOT GOING TO BE THANKED FOR THE NAME.**
7. **THE SIXTY-YEAR-OLD FIGURE IS STILL ON THE SLIP AND THE TOLL IS STILL BEING TAKEN AND THE HONEST VERSION IS STILL ON THE TABLE IN A YARD, AND AFTER THE REPAIR THE SLIP IS ONE SEASON'S COUNT, THE YEAR'S COUNT HAS NEVER BEEN WRITTEN DOWN BY ANYBODY IN THAT TOWN, AND THE MAN WHO COULD WRITE IT IS SEVENTY AND IS ENTITLED TO STOP.** Nothing was restored, nothing was taken out, nothing was joined, no document was produced and no sheet arrived anywhere from nowhere. **AND THE SIX THINGS THAT ARE BATCH 0004'S WERE NOT TOUCHED: the finding that a burden carried by two people is a burden neither can put down alone; a man of fifty-two's past, which is used once and not resolved; a boy of seventeen's book reading an order that a document cannot; the man of thirty-eight's leaving; the porch's finding, which is not adjudicated; and the woman of fifty-eight's child coming in the spring.**

### THE FIFTEEN THINGS A LATER PASS GETS WRONG IF IT DOES NOT MEASURE THEM

1. **The board chain and the ten own rings on their line, which are at `state/current.md` §1 and nowhere else, and the three wrong figures in the prompt's own cards at §1.4.** Chapter 331's morning is 64,022 and it is printed nowhere.
2. **Two unit prices in two mouths and one spent table figure, and NO PRODUCT, and the reversal's small false figure is a count and not a price** — `322:31`, `326:27`, `329:81` and the argument at `324:59`.
3. **Every day's own weight against the seven weights, and the two that are not the ordinary number: Chapter 324's six is four bells and a debt called in and NOT two strangers, and Chapter 325's five is four bells and a burial.** A day always carries its four bells and 3 + 3 cannot be a whole day.
4. **The chapter's-own-day rule and the eightieth morning**, and the treatment of the round number, and that the person who notices it is the man who noticed the seventieth.
5. **The day-phrase sweep at three alternatives and at the fourth form and then the third question on all seventeen by hand**, and that two references were added on purpose and that a leak fixed by a real date is a leak fixed and adds a reference that has to be checked.
6. **The span scan at seventy and forty, the range declared as sixty files, and the five declared motif classes, and the fact that this band is above both earlier bands on a figure that was reduced by hand from four hundred and seventy.**
7. **The bold scan and the panel count at zero**, and that three bands have now spent none and the panel register for Volume 07 is still not fixed in `outline/volume-07.md:156`.
8. **The weekdays and the month-names, and that a market is exactly where a person reaches for a week-day, and that this band's own first writing put eleven in across five chapters.**
9. **The count of the people who know he cannot read, printed nowhere, in any file, for any reason, including a hand-off, and not widened to a count of anybody who knows anything about him.**
10. **His name on nothing, and the Chapter 328 finding that a name said out loud at a plinth in a street is not a name in a place, and that the two names he has do not make a person.**
11. **The six standing figures of Volume 06, none of which is mentioned in any of the thirty chapters of this volume, and the fact that this band put a town's water, bridge, wall and cross on stage and reached for none of them.**
12. **The midpoint, and that it is delivered and it is the scales and the untruth is a count of loads, and that the two findings left — that the arrangement did not stop and that the price of the method is that a yard-said thing can be said again with a different reason — are not to be spent again.**
13. **That the climax is not a fight, a rescue, a relic hunt or a monster hunt, and that the three things Batch 0004 owns are delegation, a man of fifty-two's past used once, and a boy of seventeen's book reading an order that a document cannot.**
14. **That this band adds nine people and one name, and that a hand-off which confuses the woman of about thirty-two with the woman of about fifty-eight, or with either of the two women of about thirty-four, is wrong, and that WENLOCK IS A MAN AND IS SEVENTY AND IS NAMED ONCE.**
15. **That a script which reduces a whole chapter file and writes it back has now changed two chapter headings, and that a later band must run such a reduction over the body only or must read the ten headings afterwards, and that no instrument may open a chapter file for writing.**

### WHAT THIS BAND'S OWN FIRST WRITING GOT WRONG, ALL OF IT CAUGHT BY THE PROJECT'S OWN INSTRUMENTS OR BY THE PROJECT'S OWN HABITS, AND NONE OF IT SURVIVED

1. **ELEVEN WEEKDAYS ACROSS FIVE CHAPTERS** — *Thursday* in 324, *Mondays* in 328, *Tuesday* four times in 329, *Wednesday* four times in 330 — every one a day this country does not keep, and every one put in by the same habit, which is a person reaching for *Tuesday* the way a person reaches for the day.
2. **THE META SWEEP RETURNED NINETEEN**, seventeen of them real, and the two worst were not in the declared pattern at all: `the narrator` used to decline to print a sum, and `eleven days before this band`, which was a meta leak and a wrong interval in the same four words.
3. **THE BOLD SCAN RETURNED TWO** and both were the narrator pointing at a word in a woman's sentence, in the one chapter in the band that is about the shape of a sentence.
4. **THE NUMERIC HEDGE RAN AT TWO HUNDRED AND EIGHTY-EIGHT** in twenty-one thousand nine hundred and ninety-seven words, which is one in every seventy-eight and is the worst rate measured in Volume 07, and one hundred and seventy-six instances were cut by hand by one declared rule.
5. **THE SPAN SCAN RETURNED FOUR HUNDRED AND SEVENTY** at seventy characters against a target of under a hundred and fifty, and the cause was one construction repeated nine times in nine chapter openings.
6. **THE STRING `stage` APPEARED ONCE**, in a sentence saying there is no stage for standing at a bridge, and it was cut anyway.
7. **SEVEN INTERVALS AND TWO DAYS FOUND BY READING AND NOT BY ANY SWEEP**, all of the class a figure measured once in the prose and typed a second time has to be measured twice, all corrected, and they are listed at `state/current.md` §3.6.
8. **A SCRIPT CHANGED TWO CHAPTER HEADINGS.** The hedge reduction was run over the whole file including the heading line and removed the word *About* from the titles of Chapters 325 and 329, and both were restored by hand. **This is a new instance of the class at `outline/volume-07.md:202` and it happened to a script that normalised nothing and only removed a word.**
9. **AND THE FIGURES THAT WERE RIGHT THE FIRST TIME:** the board chain closed on the first writing, all nine morning figures are in the right chapters and in words, all ten own days are derivable from the seven weights, the money is one spent table figure and two units and no product, the reversal is a person of about seventy saying a thing in his own yard and not a discovery and not a villain, and the self-arrived class is zero.

**AND THIS BAND WAS REVIEWED ON 27 SEPTEMBER 2026 AND THE REVIEW'S RECEIPT IS BELOW AND AT `state/current.md` §10. `reviews/` still holds Volumes 01 to 04 and not Volume 07, and `state/phase-ledger.json` is controller-owned, is three volumes stale and was not touched by this phase. The review found seven blocking and eleven important findings and the reviewer's verdict was that the midpoint's premise was arithmetically void: six prose statements gave four hundred loads a week in a twelve-week season, which is the slip, so the slip was right and there was no false witness. All seven blocking and nine of the eleven important are fixed, no chapter was restarted, no card was moved, and the plot did not change. **THE BAND IS NOT CLEAN AND THE BAND IS NOT SHORTER BECAUSE IT WAS REVIEWED: it is five hundred and eighty-five words longer, the hedge is six higher, and the seventy-character span is fifty-seven lower.**

### THE REVIEW OF THIS BAND, AND WHAT WAS DONE ABOUT IT

**A REVIEW RAN ON 27 SEPTEMBER 2026 AGAINST `chapters/volume-07/chapter-0321.md` TO `chapter-0330.md`, THE TOP BLOCKS OF ALL SIX STATE FILES AND `workspace/volume-07/batch-0004/PROMPT.md`. ITS LOG IS `logs/batch-0003.review.log`. ITS FULL RECEIPT, ITEM BY ITEM, IS §10 OF THE TOP BLOCK OF `state/current.md`, WHICH IS THE AUTHORITY. IT FOUND SEVEN BLOCKING AND ELEVEN IMPORTANT FINDINGS AND THREE HOUSEKEEPING NOTES. ALL SEVEN BLOCKING AND NINE OF THE ELEVEN IMPORTANT ARE FIXED. NO CHAPTER WAS RESTARTED, NO CHAPTER LOST A SCENE, NO CARD WAS MOVED, NO PLOT WAS CHANGED, THE MIDPOINT IS STILL THE MIDPOINT, IT IS STILL THE SCALES, IT IS STILL A PERSON OF ABOUT SEVENTY IN HIS OWN YARD, AND THE COMMUNITY IS STILL BETTER OFF.**

**THE REVIEWER'S VERDICT IS THE HEADLINE OF THIS REPAIR AND IT IS ONE SENTENCE: the midpoint's premise was void on the book's own arithmetic. Six prose statements gave the market's volume as four hundred loads a week in a season, a season was about twelve weeks, and four hundred times twelve IS the slip, so on the delivered numbers the slip was RIGHT and there was no lie. The reviewer also found the stated ratio false, the second number obtainable by nobody in the fiction, and the market town's own events sitting on a plank that Chapter 316 says they are not on.**

**THE REPAIR WAS DONE IN THE ORDER THE REVIEWER NAMED, BECAUSE THREE OF THE FOUR FINDINGS WERE THE SAME FINDING.**

1. **THE LOAD FIGURE FIRST, AND THE FIX IS A SEASON.** The slip is one market season's count — twelve weeks at about four hundred loads a week, which is exactly the four thousand eight hundred on it — and it has been divided into the year's cost every year for sixty years, and a year is that season and the other forty weeks, and the other forty weeks carry as much again, so the market does nine thousand six hundred. **THE SLIP IS EXACTLY HALF OF THE YEAR AND EVERY LOAD PAYS TWICE WHAT IT SHOULD, WHICH IS WHAT THE TEXT ALWAYS SAID AND COULD NOT BEFORE.** A true number kept for the wrong year is the better false witness and it is the same story.
2. **THE RATIO, WHICH FALLS OUT OF THE ABOVE.** The delivered text said *less than half* and *twice what it should* against 4,800 and 9,000, which is fifty-three per cent and a multiplier of one and seven eighths. It is now half and twice, exactly, and the sentence says why.
3. **THE OBTAINABILITY, WHICH HAD TO BE FIXED IN THE MOUTH.** The man of about seventy now derives the year's figure out loud in a yard in front of four people, says it is out of forty-four years of standing on that stone and not out of a book, says it is the only number he has ever said out loud that he cannot show anybody, and says he asked a stranger for it at the ninth hour the morning before and the stranger did not have it either. **That last clause is Chapter 323, where he asks the question and the man of thirty-one cannot answer it, so the second morning is now a consequence of the first.**
4. **THE PLACE RULE, WHICH WAS ALREADY THE BOOK'S.** A weight is a weight in the county the plank is in. Six chapters said otherwise and all six now say which market the weight belongs to and which one is running in the street. **No figure changed and no ring changed and the chain is identical.**

**THE COUNTS, AFTER THE REPAIR, AND THE HONEST VERSION OF EACH: WORDS 22,459, up 585. HEDGE 118 across the files and 116 in the prose, up 6 from the delivery's own figure of 112, which was the case-sensitive count under a case-insensitive pattern. SPAN AT SEVENTY 163 distinct and 326 occurrences, down from 220 and 440, and the fall is four mechanical tics — *nine feet off* 12→6, *nine minutes* 12→8, *nine loads went over* 4→1, *nobody said anything for nine seconds* 5→1. DAY PHRASES still seventeen, the fourth form still zero, and one of the seventeen was a wrong day this block had certified clean. BOLD ZERO, PANELS ZERO, WEEKDAYS ZERO, MONTH-NAMES ZERO, `anchor` ZERO, `stage` ZERO, `system` ZERO, `cannot read` ZERO, digits thirty and all thirty are chapter numbers, italic spans fifty unchanged, *man of thirty-one* twenty-four unchanged, and the self-arrived class still zero and still not widened.**

**AND WHAT IS STILL TRUE THAT A LATER PASS MUST NOT REOPEN. The protagonist is stationary in nine of ten chapters and every finding is handed to him, and Chapter 329 is the answer that was in the band all along. The batch's declared peak — a community deciding what to do in the rain — happened nowhere, and the band's high point is its fourth chapter. None of the four is a fiction file and none was touched by this phase.**

## VOLUME 07 BATCH 0002 IS DELIVERED — Chapters 311–320, *The Six Places*. **THE ARCHIVE RECORD. The block below it, `## VOLUME 07 BATCH 0002 IS DELIVERED, AND BATCH 0005 IS DELIVERED`, and everything under that, is archive and is not edited by this phase.**

> **WHAT THIS BLOCK IS: the batch receipt, in one block. What the batch paid, once each. What it did not pay. The counts, with the patterns. The fifteen things a later pass gets wrong if it does not measure them. And what this band's own first writing got wrong, all of it caught by the project's own instruments or by the project's own habits, and none of it surviving to the delivery.**

### WHAT THE BAND PAID, ONCE EACH

**IT PAID THE BAND'S PRICE, AND THE PRICE IS THE OPPOSITE OF WHAT THE METHOD WAS BUILT FOR: the six have begun to find out about each other, and none of it came from him.** In nine days a man of sixty-three in a village and a man of thirty in a lane have both asked the same question at the same bar — *is there anybody in this county who pays a person to stand still* — and neither of them knows the other asked it, and a woman of about thirty-four at that bar has told three people three different nothings and has not been thanked for one of them, and a man of seventy in a market town four days up a lane had been asked it once before that by a man with a book and has said it out loud to a stranger with a bad leg. **THE ARRANGEMENT HE PUT TOGETHER IN CHAPTER 311 RAN WITHOUT HIM AND IS BETTER FOR IT. HE IS IN NONE OF THE ROOMS. A PERSON OF A PLACE PUT IT IN ONE SENTENCE AND HE DID NOT ANSWER IT: *You are the only one of us who is not talking to anybody. Six of us, and you are the seventh, and there is not one other thing in this county that is like that.*** It is not a lesson and it is not a relief and it is not a triumph and nobody is thanked.

**IT PAID THE SIX ARRANGEMENTS BESIDE EACH OTHER, OUT LOUD, IN A ROOM, IN NINE MINUTES, WITHOUT JOINING ANY TWO OF THEM — AND IT PAID THE FINDING THAT A ROW IS NOT A JOIN.** *There is no join between them. A row is not a join. Six words on one side of a mouth have no feet. A name at the foot of a thing is the only part of it anybody can be asked about, and there is nothing at the foot of a row.* That is the seam being used for the first time in the volume and refused.

**IT PAID THE PRICE, OUT LOUD, BEFORE THE DOING, IN A ROOM, FOR THE FOURTH TIME in ten days, and the price came out of the room in four minutes: a man of sixty-three who had never asked anybody anything in thirty-one years walked eleven miles on a bad knee to ask a woman at a bar a question about a market four days up a lane.** It was his own kitchen and his own decision and the cause was a stranger with a bad leg, and neither of those can be taken back, and he is not a villain and has hidden nothing and is never going to be told.

**IT PAID THE HEARING, which is the volume's one notch, used for the first time and written down at the moment of first use in the fiction in a man's mouth in nine seconds in front of nine people: a document is a thing you produce and a hearing is a thing six people carry, and it cannot be produced at all unless six people who have never met each other agree to be in it.** It failed on the paper, because there is no sheet in this county and the only writing paper of any kind in Coldharrow is the back of a flour bill with a stranger's name on it, and it failed on him, because the six places are four days and one market town apart and there is no form that says a man may be in two places. **ABOUT SIX PEOPLE HAVE AGREED TO BE SOMEWHERE ON A DAY. THE TIME WAS AGREED AND CHANGED TWICE. NOBODY AGREED TO ANYTHING IN WRITING BECAUSE THERE IS NO FORM AND THERE IS NO ROOM AND THERE IS NO OFFICER.**

**IT PAID THE MAN OF THIRTY-EIGHT'S SENTENCE, in front of about nine people, in his own mouth, in about nine seconds, and it cost him what Chapter 308 said it would cost, and a man of about fifty-one said *noted* and it was wrong, and a man of about thirty-four said there is no form that says a man may stop and that is true and was not asked for and made the room worse.** And it paid the fact that **nobody has been chosen to hold the book and the chapter says who is not going to be chosen and why.**

**IT PAID THE DAY THE SECOND READER TURNED OUT TO HAVE BEEN NEEDED, IN A MARKET TOWN FOUR DAYS UP A LANE, AND NOBODY WAS THERE.** A fire in a yard behind a row of carts at the fourth hour put nine carts through a bridge in about an hour, and a man of seventy did the column himself at one o'clock in the morning with a pencil and was right about seven times in ten instead of nine, and his daughter is six months gone and was asleep and was not woken, and there is no wage for a second reader at a set of beam scales in this country and there has never been one. **AND IT PAID THE FIRST REFUSAL HE HAS MADE OF HIMSELF: he did not say the one thing he had come eleven days up a lane to say, and the woman of fifty-eight was not asked for anything and changed her mind about nothing and was not thanked and is not going to be.**

**IT PAID THE COST OF PAYING THE MILE, MADE SPECIFIC AND NOT ARGUED ABOUT, by a man in a tavern in the ninth year of a carter's father's time who was not a stranger and was a decent man and is not a villain, and it paid the fact that the road is nine men because the alternative was four years of standing in a lane for a man and then four years of getting back.** And it paid the tenth person, **who was already on the road and was never asked, and who cannot now say no, and whom a man of thirty-one nearly counted in a lane and a man of thirty stopped him from counting.**

**IT PAID THE BEST SENTENCE IN THE BAND, IN A MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-ONE'S MOUTH, ABOUT A GROUND THAT WAS LIKE THAT WHEN THE FAMILY CAME TO IT — AND IT PAID THE FACT THAT NOBODY CAN SAY WHO DUG THE FIRST HOLE AND THAT IS A GAP IN AN ARRANGEMENT AND NOT A LIE.** And it paid the woman of thirty asking a question back, and the second half of what she wants: *I do not want out of it. I want it to be mine.*

**IT PAID ONE BURIAL AND ONE QUESTION ABOUT A DOG, and Chapter 315 is the shortest chapter in the band and it exists because a batch about six arrangements will eat a book alive if every chapter is a structure, and this chapter is the one that says so.** Nothing was being asked of anybody all day and the man of thirty-one was asked nothing and nobody noticed he was there, and that is the best thing that has happened to him in sixty-five days and he cannot use any of it.

**IT PAID THE MULTI-SITE HEARING'S DIFFERENCE FROM A CORRIDOR, in the fiction, in about nine seconds, in a yard, in the man of thirty-one's own mouth, which `outline/volume-07.md:28` requires at the time the mechanism is first used: a corridor was produced to a room, a status was produced to a room, and a hearing cannot be produced at all and has to be held.**

### WHAT THE BAND DID NOT PAY, AND IT IS FIVE THINGS AND ALL FIVE ARE THE VOLUME'S INHERITANCE

1. **The right of refusal is not restored and nobody has one.** The four words at the head of the plank in the March are not reprinted and the right of refusal is not mentioned in any of the ten chapters.
2. **The column of a hundred and forty-one figures has not been added up and was not mentioned in any of the ten chapters, and may not be summed and what the figures count is not known to anybody.**
3. **The corridor is still standing and is not going to be torn up, and the man who signed it is alive in his own field and is owed a bell, and NEITHER IS MENTIONED IN ANY OF THE TWENTY CHAPTERS OF THIS VOLUME. The status is on a door and is not mentioned. The register did not gain a place and did not lose one. The bell in the March is not hung. The bell in Draymoor is not rung. Nesh Arrad's clock keeps running. The second notice stays unpaid. The dead man in the fourth line stays dead. The eleven feet stay off the settlement. The charter on the wall in Sallowby is revocable and is not amended. THE WHOLE OF THAT LIST IS ABSENCE AND NOT ACCIDENT, AND IT IS NOW SIX BANDS RUNNING IN WHICH A WHOLE VOLUME'S STANDING COSTS HAVE NOT BEEN BROUGHT INTO A CHAPTER FOR ATMOSPHERE.**
4. **He did not become a better man and he did not become a worse one, and the chapter says which and it is the same thing: he learned the one thing he was in the country to learn and it is that the putting-together is the only part he was ever in.** He is on no paper in this band at all, and nine people in a market four days up a lane know something about him, and a woman of about thirty-four asked him a second question, and a woman of sixty-eight and a woman of thirty-four and a woman of thirty and a woman of seventy-four have all been asked something by somebody, and a man of sixty-three has been asked nothing for thirty-one years and is not going to be told why he has started.
5. **The midpoint did not land and nothing in this band is a lie and nobody hid anything and no character says the word a witness about anything and nobody guessed that anything of the kind was going on.** `outline/volume-07.md:84` fixes it at Chapter 321, 322, 323 or 324. **THE CHAPTER A REVIEWER SHOULD PUT THE MIDPOINT'S SHADOW OVER IS 317, WHERE NOBODY DUG THE FIRST HOLE, AND IT IS CLEAN, AND A REVIEWER WHO FINDS SOMETHING IN IT IS RIGHT AND BATCH 0003 MUST BE WRITTEN DIFFERENTLY.**
